When in the description of the typical modalities provided for in articles 178 or 179 any of the preceding circumstances has been taken into consideration, the conflict shall be resolved in accordance with the rule of article 8.4 of this Code.
2. If two or more of the preceding circumstances are present, the penalties respectively provided for in paragraph 1 of this article shall be imposed in their upper half.
3. In all cases provided for in this chapter, when the guilty party has taken advantage of his or her status as an authority, agent thereof or public official, the penalty of absolute disqualification from six to twelve years shall also be imposed.

CHAPTER II

Sexual assaults on minors under sixteen years of age

Article 181

1. Whoever performs acts of a sexual nature with a minor under sixteen years of age shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from two to six years.
For these purposes, acts of a sexual nature include those performed by the minor with a third party or upon himself or herself at the instigation of the perpetrator.
2. If in the conducts of the preceding paragraph any of the modalities described in article 178.2 and 3 is present, a penalty of imprisonment from five to ten years shall be imposed.
3. The sentencing body, reasoning it in the judgment, in view of the lesser seriousness of the act and assessing all the concurrent circumstances, including the personal circumstances of the guilty party, may impose the penalty of imprisonment lower in degree, except when violence or intimidation is involved or it is performed upon a victim whose will has been nullified for any reason, or the circumstances mentioned in paragraph 5 of this article are present.
4. When the sexual act consists of carnal access by vaginal, anal or oral route, or in introduction of bodily members or objects through either of the first two routes, the responsible person shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from eight to twelve years in the cases of paragraph 1, and with the penalty of imprisonment from twelve to fifteen years in the cases of paragraph 2.
5. The conducts provided for in the preceding paragraphs shall be punished with the corresponding penalty of imprisonment in its upper half when any of the following circumstances is present:
a) When the acts are committed through the joint action of two or more persons.
b) When the sexual assault is preceded or accompanied by violence of extreme gravity or by acts of a particularly degrading or humiliating nature.
c) When the acts are committed against a person who is in a situation of special vulnerability by reason of age, illness, disability or any other circumstance, and, in any case, when the person is under four years of age.
d) When the victim is or has been the partner of the perpetrator, even without cohabitation.

e) When, for the execution of the offence, the responsible person has taken advantage of a situation or relationship of cohabitation or kinship or a relationship of superiority with respect to the victim.
f) When the responsible person makes use of weapons or other equally dangerous means, capable of producing death or any of the injuries provided for in articles 149 and 150 of this Code, without prejudice to the provisions of article 194 bis.
g) When for the commission of these acts the responsible person has nullified the will of the victim by supplying pharmaceuticals, drugs or any other natural or chemical substance suitable for that purpose.
h) When the infraction has been committed within a criminal organization or group dedicated to carrying out such activities.
In the event that in the description of the typical modalities provided for in paragraphs 1 to 3 of this article any of the preceding circumstances has been taken into consideration, the conflict shall be resolved in accordance with the rule of article 8.4 of this Code.
6. If two or more of the preceding circumstances are present, the penalties of the preceding paragraph shall be imposed in their upper half.
7. In all cases provided for in this article, when the guilty party has taken advantage of his or her status as an authority, agent thereof or public official, the penalty of absolute disqualification from six to twelve years shall also be imposed.

Article 182

1. Whoever, for sexual purposes, causes a minor under sixteen years of age to witness acts of a sexual nature, even if the perpetrator does not participate in them, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years.
2. If the acts of a sexual nature that are caused to be witnessed by the minor under sixteen years of age constitute an offence against sexual freedom, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one to three years.

Article 183

1. Whoever through the internet, telephone or any other information and communication technology contacts a minor under sixteen years of age and proposes to arrange a meeting with him or her in order to commit any of the offences described in articles 181 and 189, provided that such proposal is accompanied by material acts aimed at approaching, shall be punished with the penalty of one to three years of imprisonment or a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, without prejudice to the penalties corresponding to the offences committed, if any. The penalties shall be imposed in their upper half when the approach is obtained by means of coercion, intimidation or deception.
2. Whoever, through the internet, telephone or any other information and communication technology contacts a minor under sixteen years of age and performs acts aimed at enticing him or her to provide pornographic material or show him or her pornographic images in which a minor is represented or appears, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years.

Article 183 bis

Except in cases where any of the circumstances provided for in the second paragraph of article 178 is present, the free consent of the minor under sixteen years of age shall exclude criminal liability for the offences provided for in this chapter when the perpetrator is a person close to the minor in age and degree of physical and psychological development or maturity.

CHAPTER III

Sexual harassment

Article 184

1.	Whoever solicits favors of a sexual nature, for himself or for a third party, within the scope of an employment, teaching, service provision or similar relationship, whether continuous or habitual, and with such conduct causes the victim an objectively and seriously

intimidating, hostile or humiliating situation, shall be punished, as the perpetrator of sexual harassment, with a prison sentence of six to twelve months or a fine of ten to fifteen months and special disqualification from the exercise of the profession, trade or activity for twelve to fifteen months.
2.	If the person guilty of sexual harassment had committed the act by taking advantage of a situation of employment, teaching or hierarchical superiority, or over a person subject to his or her guardianship or custody, or with the express or tacit announcement of causing the victim harm related to the legitimate expectations that the victim may have within the scope of the indicated relationship, the penalty shall be imprisonment of one to two years and special disqualification from the exercise of the profession, trade or activity for eighteen to twenty-four months.
3.	Likewise, if the person guilty of sexual harassment had committed it in centers for the protection or reform of minors, detention centers for foreign nationals, or any other center of detention, custody or reception, even of temporary stay, the penalty shall be imprisonment of one to two years and special disqualification from the exercise of the profession, trade or activity for eighteen to twenty-four months, without prejudice to the provisions of article 443.2.
4.	When the victim is in a situation of special vulnerability by reason of age, illness or disability, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half.
5.	When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis, a legal person is responsible for this offense, it shall be imposed a fine of six months to two years. Having regard to the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

CHAPTER IV

On the offenses of exhibitionism and sexual provocation

Article 185

Whoever executes or causes another person to execute acts of obscene exhibition before minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to one year or a fine of 12 to 24 months.

Article 186

Whoever, by any direct means, sells, disseminates or exhibits pornographic material among minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to one year or a fine of 12 to 24 months.

CHAPTER V

On offenses relating to prostitution and the sexual exploitation and corruption of minors.

Article 187

1.	Whoever, using violence, intimidation or deception, or abusing a situation of superiority or of need or vulnerability of the victim, causes a person of legal age to engage in or remain in prostitution, shall be punished with prison sentences of two to five years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.
A prison sentence of two to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed on whoever profits by exploiting the prostitution of another person, even with the consent of that person. In all cases, it shall be understood that there is exploitation when any of the following circumstances exist:
a)	That the victim is in a situation of personal or economic vulnerability.
b)	That onerous, disproportionate or abusive conditions are imposed for its exercise.
2.	The penalties provided for in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed in their upper half, in their respective cases, when any of the following circumstances exist:

a)	When the offender had taken advantage of his or her status as an authority, agent thereof or public official. In this case, the penalty of absolute disqualification for six to twelve years shall also be applied.
b)	When the offender belonged to a criminal organization or group engaged in carrying out such activities.
c)	When the offender had endangered, intentionally or through gross negligence, the life or health of the victim.
3.	The penalties indicated shall be imposed in their respective cases without prejudice to those corresponding to the sexual assaults or abuses committed against the prostituted person.

Article 188

1.	Whoever induces, promotes, favors or facilitates the prostitution of a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection, or profits from it, or exploits in any other way a minor or a person with a disability for these purposes, shall be punished with prison sentences of two to five years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.
If the victim were under sixteen years of age, a prison sentence of four to eight years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed.
2.	If the acts described in the preceding paragraph were committed with violence or intimidation, in addition to the fines provided, a prison sentence of five to ten years shall be imposed if the victim is under sixteen years of age, and a prison sentence of four to six years in other cases.
3.	Penalties higher in degree than those provided for in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed, in their respective cases, when any of the following circumstances exist:
a)	When the victim is in a situation of special vulnerability by reason of age, illness, disability or any other circumstance.
b)	When, for the execution of the offense, the person responsible had taken advantage of a situation of cohabitation or a relationship of superiority or kinship, by being an ascendant, or sibling, by nature or adoption, or relatives by affinity, with the victim.
c)	When, for the execution of the offense, the person responsible had taken advantage of his or her status as an authority, agent thereof or public official. In this case, a penalty of absolute disqualification for six to twelve years shall also be imposed.
d)	When the offender had endangered, intentionally or through gross negligence, the life or health of the victim.
e)	When the acts had been committed by the joint action of two or more persons.
f)	When the offender belonged to an organization or association, even of a transitory nature, engaged in carrying out such activities.
4.	Whoever solicits, accepts or obtains, in exchange for remuneration or promise, a sexual relationship with a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to four years. If the minor had not reached sixteen years of age, a prison sentence of two to six years shall be imposed.
5.	The penalties indicated shall be imposed in their respective cases without prejudice to those corresponding to the offenses against sexual freedom or indemnity committed against minors and persons with disabilities in need of special protection.

Article 189

1.	The following shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years:
a)	Whoever recruits or uses minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection for purposes of or in exhibitionist or pornographic shows, whether public or private, or to produce any kind of pornographic material, whatever its medium, or finances any of these activities or profits from them.

b)	Whoever produces, sells, distributes, exhibits, offers or facilitates the production, sale, dissemination or exhibition by any means of child pornography or in whose production persons with disabilities in need of special protection have been used, or possesses it for these purposes, even if the material originated abroad or its origin is unknown.
For the purposes of this Title, child pornography or pornography in whose production persons with disabilities in need of special protection have been used is considered to be:
a)	Any material that visually represents a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection participating in sexually explicit conduct, whether real or simulated.
b)	Any representation of the sexual organs of a minor or person with a disability in need of special protection for primarily sexual purposes.
c)	Any material that visually represents a person who appears to be a minor participating in sexually explicit conduct, whether real or simulated, or any representation of the sexual organs of a person who appears to be a minor, for primarily sexual purposes, unless the person who appears to be a minor was actually eighteen years of age or older at the time the images were obtained.
d)	Realistic images of a minor participating in sexually explicit conduct or realistic images of the sexual organs of a minor, for primarily sexual purposes.
2.	Those who commit the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this article shall be punished with a prison sentence of five to nine years when any of the following circumstances is present:
a)	When minors under sixteen years of age are used.
b)	When the acts are of a particularly degrading or humiliating nature, physical or sexual violence is used to obtain the pornographic material, or scenes of physical or sexual violence are depicted.
c)	When minors who are in a situation of special vulnerability by reason of illness, disability or any other circumstance are used.
d)	When the offender has endangered, intentionally or through gross negligence, the life or health of the victim.
e)	When the pornographic material is of notorious importance.
f)	When the offender belongs to an organization or association, even of a temporary nature, that is dedicated to carrying out such activities.
g)	When the person responsible is an ascendant, guardian, curator, keeper, teacher or any other person in charge, in fact, even if provisionally, or by law, of the minor or person with a disability in need of special protection, or is any person who lives with him or her or another person who has acted by abusing his or her recognized position of trust or authority.
h)	When the aggravating circumstance of recidivism is present.
3.	If the acts referred to in letter a) of the first paragraph of paragraph 1 had been committed with violence or intimidation, the penalty higher in degree than those provided for in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed.
4.	Whoever knowingly attends exhibitionist or pornographic shows in which minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection participate shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to two years.
5.	Whoever acquires or possesses child pornography or pornography in whose production persons with disabilities in need of special protection have been used, for his or her own use, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three months to one year or with a fine of six months to two years.
The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever knowingly accesses child pornography or pornography in whose production persons with disabilities in need of special protection have been used, by means of information and communication technologies.
6.	Whoever has under his or her parental authority, guardianship, custody or foster care a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection and who, with knowledge of his or her state of prostitution or corruption, does not do what is possible to prevent his or her continuation in such

state, or does not go to the competent authority for the same purpose if he or she lacks the means for the custody of the minor or person with a disability in need of special protection, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three to six months or a fine of six to twelve months.
7.	The Public Prosecutor's Office shall promote the appropriate actions in order to deprive of parental authority, guardianship, custody or foster care, as the case may be, the person who engages in any of the conduct described in the preceding paragraph.
8.	Judges and courts shall order the adoption of the necessary measures for the removal of web pages or internet applications that contain or disseminate child pornography or in whose production persons with disabilities in need of special protection have been used or, as the case may be, to block access to them for Internet users located in Spanish territory.
These measures may be agreed upon as a precautionary measure at the request of the Public Prosecutor's Office.

Article 189 bis

The distribution or public dissemination through the Internet, telephone or any other information or communication technology of content specifically intended to promote, encourage or incite the commission of the offenses provided for in this chapter and in chapters II and IV of this title shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months or a prison sentence of one to three years.
The judicial authorities shall order the adoption of the necessary measures for the removal of the content referred to in the preceding paragraph, for the interruption of services that predominantly offer such content or for the blocking of both when they are located abroad.

Article 189 ter

When in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offenses included in this Chapter, the following penalties shall be imposed:
a)	A fine of three to five times the profit obtained, if the offense committed by the natural person carries a prison sentence of more than five years.
b)	A fine of two to four times the profit obtained, if the offense committed by the natural person carries a prison sentence of more than two years not included in the preceding subparagraph.
c)	A fine of two to three times the profit obtained, in all other cases.
d)	Dissolution of the legal person, in accordance with the provisions of article 33.7 b) of this Code, and the other penalties provided therein that are compatible with dissolution may be decreed, taking into account the rules set forth in article 66 bis.

CHAPTER VI

Common provisions to the preceding chapters

Article 190

The conviction of a foreign Judge or Court, imposed for offenses included in this Title, shall be equivalent to the judgments of Spanish Judges or Courts for the purposes of applying the aggravating circumstance of recidivism.

Article 191

1.	To proceed for the crimes of sexual assault and sexual harassment, a complaint from the aggrieved person, their legal representative, or a criminal complaint from the Public Prosecutor's Office shall be required, which shall act weighing the legitimate interests present. When the victim is a minor, a person with a disability in need of special protection, or a helpless person, a complaint from the Public Prosecutor's Office shall suffice.

2.	In these crimes, the pardon of the offended party or legal representative does not extinguish the criminal action or liability of that kind.

Article 192

1.	Those convicted of a prison sentence for one or more crimes included in this Title shall also be subject to the measure of supervised release, which shall be executed following the custodial sentence. The duration of said measure shall be five to ten years, if any of the crimes were serious, and one to five years if it involves one or more less serious crimes. In the latter case, when it involves a single crime committed by a first-time offender, the court may or may not impose the measure of supervised release in consideration of the lesser dangerousness of the perpetrator.
2.	Ascendants, guardians, curators, caretakers, teachers or any other person in charge de facto or de jure of the minor or person with a disability in need of special protection, who participate as perpetrators or accomplices in the commission of the crimes included in this Title, shall be punished with the penalty corresponding to them, in its upper half.
This rule shall not apply when the circumstance contained therein is specifically contemplated in the criminal offense in question.
3.	The judicial authority shall impose on persons responsible for committing any of the crimes of Chapters I or V when the victim is a minor and in any case any of the crimes of Chapter II, in addition to the penalties provided for in such Chapters, the penalty of deprivation of parental authority or special disqualification for the exercise of the rights of parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, care or foster care, for a period of four to ten years. Persons responsible for the remaining crimes of this Title may be reasonably imposed, in addition to the penalties indicated for such crimes, the penalty of deprivation of parental authority or the penalty of special disqualification for the exercise of the rights of parental authority, guardianship, curatorship, care or foster care, for a period of six months to six years, as well as the penalty of disqualification for public employment or office or exercise of profession or trade, remunerated or not, for a period of six months to six years.
Likewise, the judicial authority shall impose on persons responsible for the crimes included in this Title, without prejudice to the penalties that correspond according to the preceding articles, a penalty of special disqualification for any profession, trade or activities, whether or not remunerated, that entails regular and direct contact with minors, for a period exceeding between five and twenty years the duration of the custodial sentence imposed in the judgment if the crime were serious, and between two and twenty years if it were less serious. In both cases, consideration shall be given proportionally to the seriousness of the crime, the number of crimes committed and the circumstances present in the convicted person.

Article 193

In convictions for crimes against sexual freedom, in addition to the pronouncement corresponding to civil liability, those that are appropriate regarding filiation and determination of maintenance shall be made, as applicable.

Article 194

In the cases typified in chapters IV and V of this title, when establishments or premises, whether or not open to the public, are used in carrying out the acts, their definitive closure shall be ordered in the conviction. The closure may also be adopted on a precautionary basis.

Article 194 bis

The penalties provided for in the crimes of this title shall be imposed without prejudice to that which may correspond for acts of physical or psychological violence that are carried out.

TITLE IX

On the omission of the duty to rescue

Article 195

1.	Whoever does not rescue a person who is helpless and in manifest and grave danger, when they could do so without risk to themselves or to third parties, shall be punished with a fine of three to twelve months.
2.	The same penalties shall be incurred by whoever, prevented from providing rescue, does not urgently request assistance from others.
3.	If the victim were so by accident caused fortuitously by the person who omitted assistance, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to 18 months, and if the accident were due to recklessness, that of imprisonment of six months to four years.

Article 196

The professional who, being obliged to do so, denies health care or abandons health services, when grave risk to the health of persons derives from the denial or abandonment, shall be punished with the penalties of the preceding article in their upper half and with special disqualification for public employment or office, profession or trade, for a period of six months to three years.

TITLE X

Crimes against privacy, the right to one's own image and the inviolability of the home

CHAPTER I

On the discovery and disclosure of secrets

Article 197

1.	Whoever, in order to discover the secrets or violate the privacy of another, without their consent, seizes their papers, letters, email messages or any other documents or personal effects, intercepts their telecommunications or uses technical devices for listening, transmission, recording or reproduction of sound or image, or of any other communication signal, shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.
2.	The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever, without being authorized, seizes, uses or modifies, to the detriment of a third party, reserved data of a personal or family nature of another that are recorded in files or computer, electronic or telematic media, or in any other type of public or private archive or register. The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever, without being authorized, accesses them by any means and on whoever alters or uses them to the detriment of the owner of the data or of a third party.
3.	The penalty of imprisonment from two to five years shall be imposed if the data or facts discovered or the images captured referred to in the preceding numbers are disseminated, revealed or transferred to third parties.
Whoever, with knowledge of their illicit origin and without having taken part in their discovery, carries out the conduct described in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.
4.	The acts described in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this article shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from three to five years when:
a)	They are committed by the persons in charge of or responsible for the files, computer, electronic or telematic media, archives or registers; or
b)	they are carried out through the unauthorized use of personal data of the victim.

If the reserved data had been disseminated, transferred or revealed to third parties, the penalties shall be imposed in their upper half.
5.	Likewise, when the acts described in the preceding paragraphs affect personal data that reveal ideology, religion, beliefs, health, racial origin or sexual life, or the victim is a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection, the penalties provided for shall be imposed in their upper half.
6.	If the acts are carried out for profit, the penalties respectively provided for in paragraphs 1 to 4 of this article shall be imposed in their upper half. If they also affect data of those mentioned in the preceding paragraph, the penalty to be imposed shall be imprisonment from four to seven years.
7.	Whoever, without authorization of the affected person, disseminates, reveals or transfers to third parties images or audiovisual recordings of that person which they had obtained with their consent in a home or in any other place beyond the reach of the gaze of third parties, when the disclosure seriously undermines the personal privacy of that person, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from three months to one year or a fine of six to twelve months.
A fine of one to three months shall be imposed on whoever, having received the images or audiovisual recordings referred to in the preceding paragraph, disseminates, reveals or transfers them to third parties without the consent of the affected person.
In the cases of the preceding paragraphs, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half when the acts had been committed by the spouse or by a person who is or has been united to them by an analogous relationship of affection, even without cohabitation, the victim was a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection, or the acts had been committed with a profit-making purpose.

Article 197 bis

1.	Whoever by any means or procedure, violating the security measures established to prevent it, and without being duly authorized, accesses or facilitates another's access to all or part of an information system or remains in it against the will of whoever has the legitimate right to exclude them, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years.
2.	Whoever through the use of devices or technical instruments, and without being duly authorized, intercepts non-public transmissions of computer data that occur from, to or within an information system, including electromagnetic emissions from them, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from three months to two years or a fine of three to twelve months.

Article 197 ter

Whoever, without being duly authorized, produces, acquires for their use, imports or, in any way, facilitates to third parties, with the intention of facilitating the commission of any of the offenses referred to in paragraphs 1 and 2 of Article 197 or Article 197 bis, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years or a fine of three to eighteen months:
a)	a computer program, designed or adapted primarily to commit such offenses; or
b)	a computer password, an access code or similar data that allow access to all or part of an information system.

Article 197 quater

If the acts described in this Chapter had been committed within a criminal organization or group, the respectively higher penalties in degree shall be applied.

Article 197 quinquies

When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offenses included in Articles 197, 197 bis and 197 ter, the penalty of a fine of six months to two years shall be imposed. In accordance with the rules established in

Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

Article 198

The authority or public official who, outside the cases permitted by Law, without legal cause for an offense, and taking advantage of their position, carries out any of the conducts described in the preceding article, shall be punished with the penalties respectively provided for therein, in their upper half and, in addition, with that of absolute disqualification for a period of six to twelve years.

Article 199

1.	Whoever reveals secrets of others, of which they have knowledge by reason of their profession or their employment relationships, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of six to twelve months.
2.	The professional who, in breach of their obligation of secrecy or confidentiality, discloses the secrets of another person, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from one to four years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification for said profession for a period of two to six years.

Article 200

The provisions of this chapter shall be applicable to whoever discovers, reveals or transfers reserved data of legal persons, without the consent of their representatives, except as provided in other provisions of this Code.

Article 201

1.	To proceed for the offenses provided for in this Chapter, a complaint by the aggrieved person or their legal representative shall be necessary.
2.	The complaint required in the preceding paragraph shall not be necessary to proceed for the acts described in Article 198 of this Code, nor when the commission of the offense affects general interests, a plurality of persons or if the victim is a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection.
3.	The pardon of the offended party or their legal representative, as the case may be, extinguishes the criminal action without prejudice to the provisions of Article 130.1.5.º, second paragraph.

CHAPTER II

On	the	breaking	and	entering	of	dwellings,	domiciles	of	legal	persons	and establishments open to the public

Article 202

1.	A private individual who, without residing therein, enters another's dwelling or remains therein against the will of its occupant, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years.
2.	If the act is carried out with violence or intimidation, the penalty shall be imprisonment from one to four years and a fine from six to twelve months.

Article 203

1.	A person who enters against the will of its holder the domicile of a public or private legal person, professional office or bureau, or a commercial establishment or premises open to the public outside opening hours, shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine from six to ten months.
2.	A person who remains against the will of its holder, outside opening hours, in the domicile of a public or private legal person, professional office or bureau, or in a commercial establishment or premises open to the public, shall be punished with a fine from one to three months.

3.	A person who with violence or intimidation enters or remains against the will of its holder in the domicile of a public or private legal person, professional office or bureau, or in a commercial establishment or premises open to the public, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to three years.

Article 204

The authority or public official who, outside the cases permitted by law and without legal cause for an offence, commits any of the acts described in the two preceding articles, shall be punished with the penalty provided respectively therein, in its upper half, and absolute disqualification from six to twelve years.

TITLE XI

Offences against honour

CHAPTER I

On slander

Article 205

Slander is the imputation of an offence made with knowledge of its falsity or reckless disregard for the truth.

Article 206

Slander shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from six months to two years or a fine from twelve to 24 months, if disseminated with publicity and, otherwise, with a fine from six to 12 months.

Article 207

A person accused of the offence of slander shall be exempt from all penalty upon proving the criminal act that he or she imputed.

CHAPTER II

On insult

Article 208

An insult is an action or expression that injures the dignity of another person, damaging their reputation or attacking their self-esteem.
Only insults that, by their nature, effects and circumstances, are considered serious in public opinion shall constitute an offence, without prejudice to the provisions of paragraph 4 of article 173.
Insults consisting of the imputation of facts shall not be considered serious, except when they have been carried out with knowledge of their falsity or reckless disregard for the truth.

Article 209

Serious insults made with publicity shall be punished with a fine from six to fourteen months and, otherwise, with a fine from three to seven months.

Article 210

A person accused of insult shall be exempt from liability upon proving the truth of the imputations when these are directed against public officials concerning facts relating to the exercise of their duties or referring to the commission of administrative infringements.

CHAPTER III

General provisions

Article 211

Slander and insult shall be deemed to have been made with publicity when they are disseminated by means of the press, broadcasting or by any other means of similar effectiveness.

Article 212

In the cases referred to in the preceding article, the natural or legal person owning the information medium through which the slander or insult was disseminated shall be jointly and severally liable in civil law.

Article 213

If slander or insult is committed by means of price, reward or promise, the Courts shall impose, in addition to the penalties prescribed for the offences in question, the special disqualification provided for in articles 42 or 45 of this Code, for a period from six months to two years.

Article 214

If the person accused of slander or insult acknowledges before the judicial authority the falsity or lack of certainty of the imputations and retracts them, the Judge or Court shall impose the penalty immediately inferior in degree and may refrain from imposing the disqualification penalty established in the preceding article.
The Judge or Court before whom the acknowledgment is made shall order that testimony of the retraction be delivered to the offended person and, if the latter so requests, shall order its publication in the same medium in which the slander or insult was uttered, in a space identical or similar to that in which its dissemination occurred and within the time period set by the sentencing Judge or Court.

Article 215

1.	No one shall be punished for slander or insult except by virtue of a complaint by the person offended by the offence or their legal representative. Proceedings shall be instituted ex officio when the offence is directed against a public official, authority or agent thereof concerning facts relating to the exercise of their duties.
2.	No one may bring an action for slander or insult uttered in judicial proceedings without prior leave of the Judge or Court having or having had jurisdiction over them.
3.	The pardon of the offended person extinguishes the criminal action, without prejudice to the provisions of article 130.1.5, second paragraph of this Code.

Article 216

In offences of slander or insult, reparation of the damage is considered to include also the publication or dissemination of the conviction judgment, at the expense of the person convicted of such offences, at the time and in the manner that the Judge or Court considers most appropriate for that purpose, having heard both parties.

TITLE XII

Offences against family relations

CHAPTER I

On illegal marriages

Article 217

A person who contracts a second or subsequent marriage, knowing that the previous one legally subsists, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year.

Article 218

1.	A person who, in order to harm the other contracting party, celebrates an invalid marriage shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years.
2.	The offender shall be exempt from penalty if the marriage is subsequently validated.

Article 219

1.	A person who authorises a marriage in which some known cause of nullity exists or is reported in the file, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years and special disqualification for public employment or office from two to six years.
2.	If the cause of nullity is dispensable, the penalty shall be suspension from public employment or office from six months to two years.

CHAPTER II

On simulation of childbirth and alteration of paternity, status or condition of a minor

Article 220

1.	The simulation of a childbirth shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from six months to two years.
2.	The same penalty shall be imposed on a person who conceals or delivers to third parties a person under eighteen years of age in order to alter or modify their filiation.
3.	The substitution of one child for another shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from one to five years.
4.	Ascendants, by nature or by adoption, who commit the acts described in the three preceding paragraphs may also be punished with the penalty of special disqualification for the exercise of the right of parental authority that they have over the child or descendant simulated, concealed, delivered or substituted, and, where appropriate, over the rest of the children or descendants for a period from four to ten years.
5.	Substitutions of one child for another that occur in health or social-health centres through serious negligence of those responsible for their identification and custody, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year.

Article 221

1. Those who, in exchange for economic compensation, deliver to another person a child, descendant or any minor even if there is no relationship of filiation or kinship, evading the legal procedures of guardianship, fostering or adoption, with the purpose of establishing a relationship analogous to that of filiation, shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from one to five years and special disqualification from the exercise of the right of parental authority, tutorship, curatorship or guardianship for a period of four to 10 years.
2. The person who receives them and the intermediary shall be punished with the same penalty, even if the delivery of the minor had been carried out in a foreign country.
3. If the acts were committed using nurseries, schools or other premises or establishments where children are received, the guilty parties shall be imposed the penalty of

special disqualification from the exercise of the aforementioned activities for a period of two to six years and the temporary or permanent closure of the establishments may be ordered. In the case of temporary closure, the period may not exceed five years.

Article 222

The educator, physician, authority or public official who, in the exercise of their profession or position, carries out the conduct described in the two preceding articles, shall incur the penalty indicated therein and, in addition, that of special disqualification from public employment or position, profession or trade, from two to six years.
For the purposes of this article, the term physician includes doctors, midwives, nursing staff and any other person who carries out a health or socio-health activity.

CHAPTER III

Of offences against family rights and duties

Section 1

Of the breach of duties of custody and the inducement of minors to abandon their domicile

Article 223

Whoever, having in their charge the custody of a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection, does not present them to their parents or guardians without justification for doing so, when required by them, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years, without prejudice to the facts constituting another more serious offence.

Article 224

Whoever induces a minor or a person with a disability in need of special protection to abandon the family domicile, or place where they reside with the consent of their parents, tutors or guardians, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years.
The parent who induces their minor child to infringe the custody regime established by the judicial or administrative authority shall incur the same penalty.

Article 225

When the person responsible for the offences provided for in the two preceding articles returns the minor or the person with a disability in need of special protection to their domicile or residence, or deposits them in a known and safe place, without having subjected them to vexations, abuse or any criminal act, nor having endangered their life, health, physical integrity or sexual freedom, the act shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three months to one year or a fine of six to 24 months, provided that the whereabouts of the minor or the person with a disability in need of special protection has been communicated to their parents, tutors or guardians, or the absence has not exceeded 24 hours.

Section 2 Of the abduction of minors

Article 225 bis

1. The parent who without justified cause abducts their minor child shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from two to four years and special disqualification from the exercise of the right of parental authority for a period of four to ten years.
2. For the purposes of this article, the following is considered abduction:

1. The removal of a minor from their habitual place of residence without the consent of the other parent or of the persons or institutions to whom their care or custody had been entrusted.
2. The retention of a minor seriously breaching the duty established by judicial or administrative resolution.
3. When the minor is transferred outside Spain or any condition is demanded for their return, the penalty indicated in paragraph 1 shall be imposed in its upper half.
4. When the abductor has communicated the place of stay to the other parent or to the person legally responsible for their care within twenty-four hours following the abduction with the commitment of immediate return which is effectively carried out, or the absence has not exceeded said period of twenty-four hours, they shall be exempt from penalty.
If the return is made, without the communication referred to in the preceding paragraph, within fifteen days following the abduction, the penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years shall be imposed.
These periods shall be computed from the date of the report of the abduction.
5. The penalties indicated in this article shall be imposed equally on the ascendants of the minor and on the relatives of the parent up to the second degree of consanguinity or affinity who engage in the conduct described above.

Section 3

Of the abandonment of family, minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection.

Article 226

1. Whoever fails to fulfil the legal duties of assistance inherent to parental authority, tutorship, guardianship or family fostering or to provide the necessary assistance legally established for the support of their descendants, ascendants or spouse, who are in need, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or a fine of six to 12 months.
2. The Judge or Court may impose, with reasoning, on the offender the penalty of special disqualification from the exercise of the right of parental authority, tutorship, guardianship or family fostering for a period of four to ten years.

Article 227

1. Whoever fails to pay for two consecutive months or four non-consecutive months any type of economic benefit in favour of their spouse or their children, established in a judicially approved agreement or judicial resolution in cases of legal separation, divorce, declaration of nullity of marriage, filiation proceedings, or maintenance proceedings in favour of their children, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three months to one year or a fine of six to 24 months.
2. Whoever fails to pay any other economic benefit established jointly or solely in the cases provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with the same penalty.
3. Reparation of the damage arising from the offence shall always entail payment of the amounts owed.

Article 228

The offences provided for in the two preceding articles shall only be prosecuted following a complaint by the aggrieved person or their legal representative. When the latter is a minor, a person with a disability in need of special protection or a helpless person, the Public Prosecutor may also file a complaint.

Article 229

1. The abandonment of a minor or of a person with a disability in need of special protection by the person in charge of their care shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from one to two years.
2. If the abandonment is carried out by the parents, tutors or legal guardians, the penalty of imprisonment from eighteen months to three years shall be imposed.
3. The penalty of imprisonment from two to four years shall be imposed when, due to the circumstances of the abandonment, the life, health, physical integrity or sexual freedom of the minor or of the person with a disability in need of special protection has been placed in concrete danger, without prejudice to punishing the act as appropriate if it constituted another more serious offence.

Artículo 230

The temporary abandonment of a minor or of a person with a disability in need of special protection shall be punished, in the respective cases, with penalties one degree lower than those provided for in the preceding article.

Artículo 231

1.	Whoever, having charge of the upbringing or education of a minor or of a person with a disability in need of special protection, delivers him or her to a third party or to a public institution without the consent of the person who entrusted him or her to them, or of the authority, in default thereof, shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months.
2.	If the delivery has placed in concrete danger the life, health, physical integrity or sexual freedom of the minor or of the person with a disability in need of special protection, the penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years shall be imposed.

Artículo 232

1.	Those who use or lend minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection for the practice of begging, even if it is concealed, shall be punished with imprisonment from six months to one year.
2.	If for the purposes of the preceding paragraph there is trafficking in minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection, violence or intimidation is used against them, or substances harmful to their health are supplied to them, the penalty of imprisonment from one to four years shall be imposed.

Artículo 233

1.	The Judge or Court, if it deems appropriate in view of the circumstances of the minor, may impose on those responsible for the offences provided for in articles 229 to 232 the penalty of special disqualification from the exercise of parental authority or of the rights of guardianship, tutorship, curatorship or foster care for a period of four to ten years.
2.	If the guilty party held guardianship of the minor by virtue of his or her status as a public official, he or she shall also be subject to the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of two to six years.
3.	In any case, the Public Prosecutor shall request from the competent authority the appropriate measures for the proper custody and protection of the minor.

TÍTULO XIII

Offences against property and against the socio-economic order

CAPÍTULO I

Theft

Artículo 234

1.	Whoever, with intent to profit, takes movable property belonging to another without the consent of its owner shall be punished, as guilty of theft, with imprisonment from six to eighteen months if the value of what is stolen exceeds 400 euros.
2.	A fine of one to three months shall be imposed if the value of what is stolen does not exceed 400 euros, unless any of the circumstances of article 235 is present. However, in the event that the guilty party has been finally convicted of at least three offences included in this Title, even if they are of a minor nature, provided that they are of the same nature and that the cumulative amount of the offences exceeds 400 €, the penalty of paragraph 1 of this article shall be imposed.
Cancelled records or those that should be cancelled shall not be taken into account.
3.	The penalties established in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed in their upper half when in the commission of the act the alarm or security devices installed on the stolen property have been neutralised, eliminated or rendered useless, by any means.

Artículo 235

1.	Theft shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years:
1.	º When things of artistic, historical, cultural or scientific value are stolen.
2.	º When they are things of basic necessity and a situation of shortage is caused.
3.	º When they are conduits, cabling, equipment or components of electricity supply, hydrocarbon or telecommunications service infrastructures, or other things intended for the provision of services of general interest, and serious disruption is caused to them.
4.	º When they are agricultural or livestock products, or the instruments or means used to obtain them, provided that the offence is committed on agricultural or livestock holdings and serious damage is caused to them.
5.	º When it is of particular gravity, having regard to the value of the stolen items, or damage of particular significance is caused.
6.	º When it places the victim or his or her family in a serious economic situation or has been carried out by abusing their personal circumstances or their situation of helplessness, or by taking advantage of the occurrence of an accident or the existence of a risk or general danger to the community that has weakened the defence of the victim or facilitated the unpunished commission of the offence.
7.	º When at the time of committing the offence the guilty party has been finally convicted of at least three offences included in this Title, provided that they are of the same nature. Cancelled records or those that should be cancelled shall not be taken into account.
8.	º When minors under sixteen years of age are used for the commission of the offence.
9.	º When the guilty party or parties participate in the acts as members of a criminal organisation or group engaged in the commission of offences included in this Title, provided that they are of the same nature.
2.	The penalty indicated in the preceding paragraph shall be imposed in its upper half when two or more of the circumstances provided for therein are present.

Artículo 236

1.	Whoever, being the owner of a movable thing or acting with the consent of the owner, steals it from the person who lawfully possesses it, to the detriment of that person or of a third party, shall be punished with a fine of three to twelve months.

2.	If the value of the stolen thing does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

CAPÍTULO II

Robbery

Artículo 237

Guilty of the offence of robbery are those who, with intent to profit, take possession of movable property belonging to another by using force on things to access or leave the place where they are located or violence or intimidation against persons, whether when committing the offence, to protect their escape, or against those who come to the aid of the victim or who pursue them.

Artículo 238

Guilty of the offence of robbery with force on things are those who commit the act when any of the following circumstances is present:
1.	º Scaling.
2.	º Breaking through a wall, ceiling or floor, or breaking a door or window.
3.	º Breaking cupboards, chests or other kinds of closed or sealed furniture or objects, or forcing their locks or discovering their keys to steal their contents, whether at the place of the robbery or outside it.
4.	º Use of false keys.
5.	º Disabling of specific alarm or guard systems.

Artículo 239

The following shall be considered false keys:
1.	Lockpicks or other similar instruments.
2.	Legitimate keys lost by the owner or obtained by a means that constitutes a criminal offence.
3.	Any others that are not those intended by the owner to open the lock violated by the offender.
For the purposes of this article, keys include magnetic or perforated cards, remote opening controls or instruments and any other technological instrument of similar effectiveness.

Artículo 240

1.	The person guilty of robbery with force on things shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years.
2.	Imprisonment from two to five years shall be imposed when any of the circumstances provided for in article 235 is present.

Article 241

1.	Robbery committed in an inhabited dwelling, building or premises open to the public, or in any of their outbuildings, shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to five years.
If the acts had been committed in an establishment open to the public, or in any of its outbuildings, outside opening hours, a prison sentence of one to five years shall be imposed.
2.	An inhabited dwelling is considered to be any shelter that constitutes the residence of one or more persons, even if they are accidentally absent from it when the robbery takes place.
3.	Outbuildings of an inhabited dwelling or of a building or premises open to the public are considered to be its courtyards, garages and other enclosed departments or sites adjacent to the building and in interior communication with it, and with which they form a physical unit.

4.	A prison sentence of two to six years shall be imposed when the acts referred to in the preceding paragraphs are particularly serious, having regard to the manner of commission of the offence or the damage caused and, in any case, when any of the circumstances set out in Article 235 is present.

Article 242

1.	A person guilty of robbery with violence or intimidation against persons shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to five years, without prejudice to that which may correspond to the acts of physical violence committed.
2.	When the robbery is committed in an inhabited dwelling, building or premises open to the public or in any of their outbuildings, a prison sentence of three years and six months to five years shall be imposed.
3.	The penalties provided for in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed in their upper half when the offender makes use of weapons or other equally dangerous means, whether in committing the offence or to protect the escape, and when he attacks those who come to the aid of the victim or those who pursue him.
4.	Having regard to the lesser degree of violence or intimidation exercised and also assessing the other circumstances of the act, the penalty lower in degree than that provided for in the preceding paragraphs may be imposed.

CHAPTER III

On extortion

Article 243

A person who, with intent to gain, compels another, with violence or intimidation, to perform or omit an act or legal transaction to the detriment of his patrimony or that of a third party, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years, without prejudice to those that may be imposed for the acts of physical violence committed.

CHAPTER IV

On robbery and unauthorised use of vehicles

Article 244

1.	A person who takes or uses without due authorisation a motor vehicle or moped belonging to another, without intent to appropriate it, shall be punished with a penalty of community service of thirty-one to ninety days or a fine of two to twelve months, if he returns it, directly or indirectly, within a period not exceeding forty-eight hours, provided that, in no case, the penalty imposed may be equal to or greater than that which would correspond if he definitively appropriated the vehicle.
2.	If the act is carried out using force against property, the penalty shall be applied in its upper half.
3.	If restitution is not made within the specified period, the act shall be punished as theft or robbery in their respective cases.
4.	If the act is committed with violence or intimidation against persons, the penalties of Article 242 shall be imposed in all cases.

CHAPTER V

On usurpation

Article 245

1.	A person who with violence or intimidation against persons occupies immovable property or usurps a real property right belonging to another shall be imposed, in addition to the penalties incurred for the violence exercised, a prison sentence of one to two years, which shall be determined taking into account the benefit obtained and the damage caused.

2.	A person who occupies, without due authorisation, immovable property, a dwelling or buildings belonging to another that do not constitute a residence, or remains in them against the will of their owner, shall be punished with a fine of three to six months.

Article 246

1.	A person who alters boundaries or limits of towns or estates or any kind of signs or boundary markers intended to fix the limits of properties or demarcations of adjoining lands, whether in the public or private domain, shall be punished with a fine of three to eighteen months.
2.	If the benefit obtained does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

Article 247

1.	A person who, without being authorised, diverts water for public or private use from its course, or from its natural or artificial reservoir, shall be punished with a fine of three to six months.
2.	If the benefit obtained does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

CHAPTER VI

On fraud

Section 1 On swindles

Article 248

Persons who, with intent to gain, use deceit sufficient to produce error in another, inducing him to perform an act of disposition to his own detriment or that of another, commit swindle.
Those guilty of swindle shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years. For the determination of the penalty, account shall be taken of the amount defrauded, the economic loss caused to the injured party, the relations between the latter and the defrauder, the means employed by the latter and any other circumstances that serve to assess the seriousness of the offence.
If the amount defrauded does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

Article 249

1.	The following are also considered guilty of swindle and shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years:
a)	Those who, with intent to gain, by obstructing or improperly interfering with the operation of an information system or by improperly introducing, altering, deleting, transmitting or suppressing computer data or by using any other computer manipulation or similar artifice, achieve an unconsented transfer of any patrimonial asset to the detriment of another.
b)	Those who, fraudulently using credit or debit cards, travellers' cheques or any other payment instrument, material or immaterial, other than cash, or the data contained in any of them, carry out operations of any kind to the detriment of their holder or a third party.
2.	The following shall be punished with the same penalty provided for in the preceding paragraph:
a)	Those who manufacture, import, obtain, possess, transport, trade or otherwise make available to third parties devices, instruments or data or computer programs, or any other means specifically designed or adapted for the commission of the swindles provided for in this article.

b)	Those who, for fraudulent use, steal, appropriate or unlawfully acquire credit or debit cards, travellers' cheques or any other payment instrument, material or immaterial, other than cash.
3.	The penalty in its lower half shall be imposed on those who, for fraudulent use and knowing that they were obtained unlawfully, possess, acquire, transfer, distribute or make available to third parties credit or debit cards, travellers' cheques or any other payment instruments, material or immaterial, other than cash.

Article 250

1.	The crime of fraud shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of six to twelve months, when:
1.	º It relates to necessities, housing or other goods of recognized social utility.
2.	º It is perpetrated by abusing another's signature, or by stealing, concealing or rendering useless, in whole or in part, any process, file, protocol or public or official document of any kind.
3.	º It relates to property that forms part of the artistic, historical, cultural or scientific heritage.
4.	º It is especially serious, having regard to the extent of the harm and the economic situation in which it leaves the victim or their family.
5.	º The value of the fraud exceeds 50,000 euros, or affects a large number of persons.
6.	º It is committed with abuse of the personal relations existing between victim and defrauder, or the latter takes advantage of their business or professional credibility.
7.	º Procedural fraud is committed. Those who, in judicial proceedings of any kind, manipulate the evidence on which they intend to base their allegations or employ other analogous procedural fraud, causing error in the judge or court and leading them to issue a decision that harms the economic interests of the other party or of a third party, incur the same.
8.	º When committing the offense the guilty party had been finally convicted of at least three crimes included in this Chapter. Cancelled records or those that should be cancelled shall not be taken into account.
2.	If the circumstances included in numerals 4.º, 5.º, 6.º or 7.º concur with that of numeral 1.º of the preceding paragraph, the penalties of imprisonment from four to eight years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed. The same penalty shall be imposed when the value of the fraud exceeds 250,000 euros.

Article 251

The following shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from one to four years:
1.	º Whoever, falsely attributing to themselves a power of disposal over a movable or immovable thing which they lack, either because they never had it or because they have already exercised it, alienates, encumbers or leases it to another, to the detriment of the latter or of a third party.
2.	º Whoever disposes of a movable or immovable thing concealing the existence of any charge upon it, or whoever, having alienated it as free, encumbers or alienates it again before the definitive transfer to the acquirer, to the detriment of the latter or of a third party.
3.	º Whoever grants a simulated contract to the detriment of another.

Article 251 bis

When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is liable for the crimes included in this Section, the following penalties shall be imposed:
a)	A fine of three to five times the amount defrauded, if the crime committed by the natural person carries a penalty of imprisonment of more than five years.

b)	A fine of two to four times the amount defrauded, in all other cases.
Having regard to the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

Section 2 Disloyal administration

Article 252

1.	Those who, having powers to administer another's patrimony, emanating from the law, entrusted by the authority or assumed by means of a legal transaction, infringe them by exceeding the exercise thereof and, in that manner, cause harm to the patrimony administered, shall be punished with the penalties of Article 248 or, as the case may be, those of Article 250.
2.	If the amount of the patrimonial harm does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

Section 2 bis Misappropriation

Article 253

1.	Those who, to the detriment of another, appropriate for themselves or for a third party, money, effects, securities or any other movable thing, which they had received on deposit, commission, or custody, or which had been entrusted to them by virtue of any other title that produces the obligation to deliver or return them, or deny having received them, shall be punished with the penalties of Article 248 or, as the case may be, of Article 250, unless they were already punished with a more severe penalty in another provision of this Code.
2.	If the amount of what is appropriated does not exceed 400 euros, a penalty of a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

Article 254

1.	Whoever, outside the cases of the preceding article, appropriates a movable thing belonging to another, shall be punished with a penalty of a fine of three to six months. If it concerns things of artistic, historical, cultural or scientific value, the penalty shall be imprisonment from six months to two years.
2.	If the amount of what is appropriated does not exceed 400 euros, a penalty of a fine of one to two months shall be imposed.

Section 3 Fraud of electric current and analogous offenses

Article 255

1.	Whoever commits fraud by using electric energy, gas, water, telecommunications or another element, energy or fluid belonging to another, by any of the following means, shall be punished with the penalty of a fine of three to twelve months:
1.	º By making use of mechanisms installed to carry out the fraud.
2.	º By maliciously altering the indications or metering devices.
3.	º By employing any other clandestine means.
2.	If the amount defrauded does not exceed 400 euros, a penalty of a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

Article 256

1.	Whoever makes use of any telecommunications terminal equipment, without the consent of its owner, and causing the latter economic harm, shall be punished with the penalty of a fine of three to twelve months.
2.	If the amount of the harm caused does not exceed 400 euros, a penalty of a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.

CHAPTER VII

Frustration of execution

Article 257

1.	The following shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months:
1.	º Whoever absconds with their property to the detriment of their creditors.
2.	º Whoever with the same purpose carries out any act of patrimonial disposal or generating obligations that delays, hinders or prevents the effectiveness of an attachment or of an enforcement or collection procedure, judicial, extrajudicial or administrative, initiated or of foreseeable initiation.
2.	Whoever carries out acts of disposal, contracts obligations that diminish their patrimony or conceals by any means elements of their patrimony upon which execution could be effected, with the purpose of evading payment of civil liabilities arising from a crime that they had committed or for which they should be liable, shall be punished with the same penalty.
3.	The provisions of this article shall apply whatever the nature or origin of the obligation or debt whose satisfaction or payment is sought to be evaded, including the economic rights of workers, and regardless of whether the creditor is a private individual or any legal person, public or private.
Notwithstanding the foregoing, in the event that the debt or obligation sought to be evaded is of public law and the creditor is a public legal person, or it concerns pecuniary obligations arising from the commission of a crime against the Public Treasury or Social Security, the penalty to be imposed shall be imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.
4.	The penalties provided for in this article shall be imposed in their upper half in the cases provided for in numerals 5.º or 6.º of paragraph 1 of Article 250.
5.	This crime shall be prosecuted even when insolvency proceedings are initiated after its commission.

Article 258

1.	Anyone who, in judicial or administrative enforcement proceedings, submits to the authority or official in charge of the enforcement an incomplete or false statement of assets or estate, and thereby delays, hinders or prevents the satisfaction of the creditor, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three months to one year or a fine of six to eighteen months.
The statement of assets or estate shall be considered incomplete when the debtor under enforcement uses or enjoys assets owned by third parties and does not provide sufficient justification of the right supporting such enjoyment and the conditions to which it is subject.
2.	The same penalty shall be imposed when the debtor, upon being required to do so, fails to provide the statement of assets or estate referred to in the preceding paragraph.
3.	The offences referred to in this article shall not be prosecutable if the perpetrator, before the authority or official had discovered the false or incomplete nature of the statement submitted, appeared before them and submitted a true and complete statement of assets or estate.

Article 258 bis

Anyone who makes use of assets seized by public authority that have been placed in deposit without being authorised to do so shall be punished with a prison sentence of three to six months or a fine of six to twenty-four months, unless they were already punished with a more severe penalty under another provision of this Code.

Article 258 ter

When, in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis, a legal person is liable for the offences included in this Chapter, the following penalties shall be imposed:

a)	Fine of two to five years, if the offence committed by the natural person carries a prison sentence of more than five years.
b)	Fine of one to three years, if the offence committed by the natural person carries a prison sentence of more than two years not included in the preceding subparagraph.
c)	Fine of six months to two years, in all other cases.
Having regard to the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b to g of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

CHAPTER VII BIS

On punishable insolvencies

Article 259

1.	Anyone who, being in a situation of actual or imminent insolvency, carries out any of the following conducts shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to four years and a fine of eight to twenty-four months:
1.	ª Conceals, damages or destroys assets or patrimonial elements that are included, or would have been included, in the insolvency estate at the time of its opening.
2.	ª Carries out acts of disposal by means of the delivery or transfer of money or other patrimonial assets, or by means of the assumption of debts, that are not proportionate to the debtor's patrimonial situation or income, and that lack economic or business justification.
3.	ª Carries out sales transactions or service provisions at a price lower than their acquisition or production cost, and which in the circumstances of the case lack economic justification.
4.	ª Simulates third-party credits or proceeds to recognise fictitious credits.
5.	ª Participates in speculative business, when this lacks economic justification and proves, in the circumstances of the case and in view of the economic activity carried out, contrary to the duty of diligence in the management of economic affairs.
6.	ª Fails to comply with the legal duty to keep accounts, keeps double accounts, or commits irregularities in their keeping that are relevant to the understanding of their patrimonial or financial situation. The destruction or alteration of accounting books shall also be punishable when this hinders or prevents in a relevant manner the understanding of their patrimonial or financial situation.
7.	ª Conceals, destroys or alters the documentation that the entrepreneur is obliged to preserve before the expiry of the period to which this legal duty extends, when this hinders or makes impossible the examination or assessment of the debtor's actual economic situation.
8.	ª Prepares the annual accounts or accounting books in a manner contrary to the regulations governing commercial accounting, in such a way as to hinder or make impossible the examination or assessment of the debtor's actual economic situation, or fails to comply with the duty to prepare the balance sheet or inventory within the time limit.
9.	ª Carries out any other active or omissive conduct that constitutes a serious breach of the duty of diligence in the management of economic affairs and to which a decrease in the debtor's estate is attributable or by means of which the debtor's actual economic situation or business activity is concealed.
2.	The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who, by means of any of the conducts referred to in the preceding paragraph, causes their situation of insolvency.
3.	When the acts have been committed through negligence, a prison sentence of six months to two years or a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed.
4.	This offence shall only be prosecutable when the debtor has ceased to regularly meet their enforceable obligations or their insolvency has been declared.
5.	This offence and the individual offences related to it, committed by the debtor or person who has acted on their behalf, may be prosecuted without waiting for the conclusion of the

insolvency proceedings and without prejudice to the continuation thereof. The amount of civil liability arising from such offences must be incorporated, where appropriate, into the estate.
6.	In no case shall the classification of the insolvency in the insolvency proceedings be binding on the criminal jurisdiction.

Article 259 bis

The acts referred to in the preceding article shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to six years and a fine of eight to twenty-four months, when any of the following circumstances is present:
1.	ª When patrimonial harm is caused or may be caused to a generality of persons or may place them in a serious economic situation.
2.	ª When economic harm exceeding
600,000 euros is caused to any of the creditors.
3.	ª When at least half of the amount of insolvency credits has as holders the Public Treasury, whether state, regional, local or provincial, and Social Security.

Article 260

1.	The debtor who, being in a situation of actual or imminent insolvency, favours one of the creditors by carrying out an act of patrimonial disposal or generating obligations intended to pay a non-enforceable credit or to provide them with a guarantee to which they were not entitled, when it is a transaction that lacks economic or business justification, shall be punished with a sentence of six months to three years' imprisonment or a fine of eight to twenty-four months.
2.	The debtor who, once the application for insolvency has been admitted for processing, without being authorised to do so either judicially or by the insolvency administrators, and outside the cases permitted by law, carries out any act of patrimonial disposal or generating obligations, intended to pay one or more creditors, whether privileged or not, with postponement of the rest, shall be punished with a sentence of one to four years' imprisonment and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

Article 261

Anyone who in insolvency proceedings knowingly submits false data relating to the accounting status, with the purpose of unduly obtaining the declaration thereof, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to two years and a fine of six to 12 months.

Article 261 bis

When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is liable for the offences included in this Chapter, the following penalties shall be imposed:
a) Fine of two to five years, if the offence committed by the natural person carries a penalty of imprisonment of more than five years.
b) Fine of one to three years, if the offence committed by the natural person carries a penalty of imprisonment of more than two years not included in the preceding paragraph.
c) Fine of six months to two years, in all other cases.
