Having regard to the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

CHAPTER VIII

On the alteration of prices in public tenders and auctions

Article 262

1. Those who solicit gifts or promises not to take part in a public tender or auction; those who attempt to drive bidders away from it by means of threats, gifts, promises or any other artifice; those who collude with each other for the purpose of altering the price of the award, or those who fraudulently abandon or withdraw from the auction having obtained the award, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of one to three years and a fine of 12 to 24 months, as well as special disqualification from bidding in judicial auctions for between three and five years. If it concerns a tender or auction called by the Administrations or public entities, the penalty of special disqualification shall also be imposed on the agent and on the person or company represented by him, which shall include, in any case, the right to contract with the public Administrations for a period of three to five years.
2. The judge or court may impose one or more of the consequences provided for in Article 129 if the offender belongs to any society, organisation or association, even of a temporary nature, engaged in carrying out such activities.
3. Current and former directors, de facto or de jure administrators, managers and other members of staff of any company, incorporated or in formation, who in that capacity have committed any of the acts provided for in this article, shall be exempt from criminal liability when they put an end to their participation therein and cooperate with the competent authorities in a full, continuous and diligent manner, providing information and evidence which the authorities did not possess, which is useful for the investigation, detection and punishment of the other persons involved, provided that the following conditions are met:
a) They actively cooperate in this regard with the competition authority handling the case,
b) these companies or natural persons have submitted an application for exemption from payment of the fine in accordance with the provisions of the Competition Defence Act,
c) such application has been submitted at a time prior to that at which the current or former directors, de facto or de jure administrators, managers and other members of staff of the company, incorporated or in formation, have been informed that they are being investigated in relation to these acts,
d) it concerns active cooperation also with the judicial authority or the Public Prosecutor's Office, providing useful and concrete evidence to secure proof of the offence and identify other perpetrators.

CHAPTER IX

On damage

Article 263

1. Anyone who causes damage to another's property not covered by other titles of this Code shall be punished with a fine of six to twenty-four months, having regard to the economic condition of the victim and the amount of the damage.
If the amount of the damage caused does not exceed 400 euros, a fine of one to three months shall be imposed.
2. Anyone who causes the damage referred to in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of one to three years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months if any of the following circumstances apply:
1. º That they are carried out to prevent the free exercise of authority or as a consequence of actions executed in the exercise of their functions, whether the offence is committed against public officials or against private individuals who, as witnesses or in any other way, have contributed or may contribute to the execution or application of the Laws or general provisions.

2. º That infection or contagion of livestock is caused by any means.
3. º That poisonous or corrosive substances are used.
4. º That they affect property in the public or communal domain or use.
5. º That they ruin the injured party or place him in a serious economic situation.
6. º That damage of particular gravity has been caused or general interests have been affected.

Article 264

1. Anyone who by any means, without authorisation and in a serious manner erases, damages, deteriorates, alters, suppresses or makes inaccessible computer data, computer programs or electronic documents belonging to others, when the result produced is serious, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of six months to three years.
2. A penalty of imprisonment of two to five years and a fine of the equivalent to ten times the damage caused shall be imposed when the conduct described is accompanied by any of the following circumstances:
1. ª It has been committed within the framework of a criminal organisation.
2. ª It has caused damage of particular gravity or affected a large number of computer systems.
3. ª The act has seriously harmed the functioning of essential public services or the provision of basic necessities.
4. ª The acts have affected the computer system of a critical infrastructure or have created a situation of serious danger to the security of the State, of the European Union or of a Member State of the European Union. For these purposes, critical infrastructure shall be considered to be an element, system or part thereof that is essential for the maintenance of vital functions of society, health, safety, protection and the economic and social welfare of the population whose disruption or destruction would have a significant impact by being unable to maintain its functions.
5. ª The offence has been committed using any of the means referred to in Article 264 ter.
If the acts have been of extreme gravity, the penalty may be imposed in the higher degree.
3. The penalties provided for in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed, in their respective cases, in their upper half, when the acts have been committed through the unlawful use of another person's personal data to facilitate access to the computer system or to gain the trust of a third party.

Article 264 bis

1. Anyone who, without being authorised and in a serious manner, obstructs or interrupts the operation of another person's computer system shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years:
a) by performing any of the conducts referred to in the preceding article;
b) by introducing or transmitting data; or
c) by destroying, damaging, rendering useless, eliminating or replacing a computer, telematic or electronic information storage system.
If the facts have significantly harmed the normal activity of a company, business or a Public Administration, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half, and the penalty may reach the next degree up.
2. A prison sentence of three to eight years and a fine of three to ten times the damage caused shall be imposed when any of the circumstances of paragraph 2 of the preceding article are present in the facts referred to in the preceding paragraph.
3. The penalties provided for in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed, in their respective cases, in their upper half, when the facts have been committed through the unlawful use of another person's personal data to facilitate access to the computer system or to gain the trust of a third party.

Article 264 ter

Anyone who, without being duly authorised, produces, acquires for their use, imports or, in any way, provides to third parties, with the intention of facilitating the commission of any of the offences referred to in the two preceding articles, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to two years or a fine of three to eighteen months:
a) a computer program, designed or adapted primarily to commit any of the offences referred to in the two preceding articles; or
b) a computer password, an access code or similar data that allow access to all or part of an information system.

Article 264 quater

When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offences included in the three preceding articles, the following penalties shall be imposed:
a) A fine of two to five years or five to twelve times the value of the damage caused, if it results in a higher amount, when dealing with offences punished with a prison sentence of more than three years.
b) A fine of one to three years or three to eight times the value of the damage caused, if it results in a higher amount, in all other cases.
Having regard to the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

Article 265

Anyone who destroys, seriously damages, or renders unfit for service, even temporarily, works, establishments or military installations, warships, military aircraft, military means of transport or transmission, war material, supplies or other means or resources assigned to the service of the Armed Forces or the Security Forces and Corps, shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to four years if the damage caused exceeds one thousand euros.

Article 266

1. Anyone who commits the damage provided for in paragraph 1 of Article 263 by means of fire, or by causing explosions, or by using any other means of similar destructive power or that generates a significant risk of explosion or of causing other damage of special seriousness, or by endangering the life or physical integrity of persons, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to three years.
2. Anyone who commits the damage provided for in paragraph 2 of Article 263, in any of the circumstances mentioned in the preceding paragraph, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three to five years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.
3. Anyone who commits the damage provided for in Articles 265, 323 and 560, in any of the circumstances mentioned in paragraph 1 of this article, shall be punished with a prison sentence of four to eight years.
4. In any of the cases provided for in the preceding paragraphs, when the damage is committed with the causing of explosions or the use of other means of similar destructive power and, in addition, the life or physical integrity of persons is endangered, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half.
In case of fire, the provisions of Article 351 shall apply.

Article 267

Damage caused by serious negligence in an amount exceeding 80,000 euros shall be punished with a fine of three to nine months, having regard to the importance thereof.
The offences referred to in this article shall only be prosecutable upon complaint by the aggrieved person or their legal representative. The Public Prosecutor may also

file a complaint when the person is a minor, a person with a disability in need of special protection or a helpless person.
In these cases, the pardon of the offended person extinguishes the criminal action.

CHAPTER X

Common provisions to the preceding chapters

Article 268

1. Spouses who are not legally or de facto separated or in judicial proceedings for separation, divorce or annulment of their marriage and ascendants, descendants and siblings by nature or by adoption, as well as relatives by affinity in the first degree if they live together, are exempt from criminal liability and subject only to civil liability for property offences that they cause to each other, provided that there is no violence or intimidation, or abuse of the victim's vulnerability, whether by reason of age, or because they are a person with a disability.
2. This provision does not apply to strangers who participate in the offence.

Article 269

Incitement, conspiracy and proposal to commit the offences of robbery, extortion, fraud or misappropriation shall be punished with a penalty one or two degrees lower than that of the corresponding offence.

CHAPTER XI

Offences relating to intellectual and industrial property, the market and consumers

Section 1 Offences relating to intellectual property

Article 270

1.	Anyone who, with the intention of obtaining a direct or indirect economic benefit and to the detriment of a third party, reproduces, plagiarises, distributes, publicly communicates or in any other way economically exploits, in whole or in part, a literary, artistic or scientific work or performance, or its transformation, interpretation or artistic execution fixed in any type of medium or communicated through any means, without the authorisation of the holders of the corresponding intellectual property rights or their assignees, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.
2.	The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who, in the provision of information society services, with the intention of obtaining a direct or indirect economic benefit, and to the detriment of a third party, actively and non-neutrally facilitates, without being limited to a merely technical processing, access to or location on the internet of works or performances subject to intellectual property without the authorisation of the holders of the corresponding rights or their assignees, in particular by offering ordered and classified lists of links to the works and content referred to above, even if such links were initially provided by the recipients of their services.
3.	In these cases, the judge or court shall order the removal of the works or performances subject to the infringement. When content subject to the intellectual property referred to in the preceding paragraphs is disseminated exclusively or predominantly through an internet access portal or information society service, the interruption of the provision thereof shall be ordered, and the judge may adopt any precautionary measure aimed at protecting intellectual property rights.
Exceptionally, when there is repetition of the conduct and when it proves to be a proportionate, efficient and effective measure, blocking of the corresponding access may be ordered.

4.	In the cases referred to in paragraph 1, itinerant or merely occasional distribution or marketing shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years.
However, taking into account the characteristics of the offender and the reduced amount of the economic benefit obtained or that could have been obtained, provided that none of the circumstances of Article 271 is present, the Judge may impose a fine of one to six months or community service of thirty-one to sixty days.
5.	The following shall be punished with the penalties provided for in the preceding paragraphs, in their respective cases:
a)	Those who intentionally export or store copies of the works, productions or performances referred to in the first two paragraphs of this article, including digital copies thereof, without the aforementioned authorisation, when they are intended to be reproduced, distributed or publicly communicated.
b)	Those who intentionally import these products without such authorisation, when they are intended to be reproduced, distributed or publicly communicated, whether they have a lawful or unlawful origin in their country of origin; however, the importation of the said products from a State belonging to the European Union shall not be punishable when they have been acquired directly from the holder of the rights in that State, or with their consent.
c)	Those who favour or facilitate the performance of the conduct referred to in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this article by removing or modifying, without authorisation from the holders of the intellectual property rights or their assignees, the effective technological measures incorporated by them for the purpose of preventing or restricting their performance.
d)	Those who, with the intention of obtaining a direct or indirect economic benefit, for the purpose of facilitating third parties' access to a copy of a literary, artistic or scientific work, or to its transformation, interpretation or artistic execution, fixed in any type of medium or communicated through any means, and without authorisation from the holders of the intellectual property rights or their assignees, circumvent or facilitate the circumvention of the effective technological measures arranged to prevent it.
6.	Anyone who manufactures, imports, puts into circulation or possesses for commercial purposes any means primarily conceived, produced, adapted or made to facilitate the unauthorised removal or neutralisation of any technical device that has been used to protect computer programs or any of the other works, interpretations or performances in the terms provided for in the first two paragraphs of this article shall also be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to three years.

Article 271

The penalty of imprisonment from two to six years, a fine of eighteen to thirty-six months and special disqualification from exercising the profession related to the offence committed, for a period of two to five years, shall be imposed when the offence of the preceding article is committed with any of the following circumstances:
a)	That the benefit obtained or that could have been obtained has special economic significance.
b)	That the facts are especially serious, taking into account the value of the unlawfully produced objects, the number of works, or of the transformation, execution or interpretation thereof, unlawfully reproduced, distributed, communicated to the public or made available to it, or the special importance of the damage caused.
c)	That the offender belongs to an organisation or association, even of a temporary nature, whose purpose is to carry out activities infringing intellectual property rights.
d)	That persons under 18 years of age are used to commit these offences.

Article 272

1.	The extent of civil liability arising from the offences defined in the two preceding articles shall be governed by the provisions of the Intellectual Property Law relating to the cessation of unlawful activity and compensation for damages.

2.	In the event of a conviction, the Judge or Court may order its publication, at the infringer's expense, in an official newspaper.

Section 2 Offences relating to industrial property

Article 273

1.	Anyone who, for industrial or commercial purposes, without the consent of the holder of a patent or utility model and with knowledge of its registration, manufactures, imports, possesses, uses, offers or introduces into commerce objects protected by such rights shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of 12 to 24 months.
2.	The same penalties shall be imposed on anyone who, in the same manner, and for the aforementioned purposes, uses or offers the use of a process that is the subject of a patent, or possesses, offers, introduces into commerce, or uses the product directly obtained by the patented process.
3.	Anyone who performs any of the acts defined in the first paragraph of this article under the same circumstances in relation to objects protected in favour of a third party by an industrial or artistic model or design or topography of a semiconductor product shall be punished with the same penalties.

Article 274

1.	Anyone who, for industrial or commercial purposes, without the consent of the holder of an industrial property right registered in accordance with trademark legislation and with knowledge of the registration,
a)	manufactures, produces or imports products that incorporate a distinctive sign identical or confusingly similar to that one, or
b)	offers, distributes, or markets wholesale products that incorporate a distinctive sign identical or confusingly similar to that one, or stores them for that purpose, when they are the same or similar products, services or activities for which the industrial property right is registered, shall be punished with the penalties of one to four years' imprisonment and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.
2.	Anyone who, for industrial or commercial purposes, without the consent of the holder of an industrial property right registered in accordance with trademark legislation and with knowledge of the registration, offers, distributes or markets retail, or provides services or carries out activities, that incorporate a distinctive sign identical or confusingly similar to that one, when they are the same or similar products, services or activities for which the industrial property right is registered, shall be punished with the penalties of six months to three years' imprisonment.
The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who reproduces or imitates a distinctive sign identical or confusingly similar to that one for use in committing the conduct punished in this article.
3.	Street or occasional sale of the products referred to in the preceding paragraphs shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment of six months to two years.
However, taking into account the characteristics of the offender and the small amount of the economic benefit obtained or that could have been obtained, provided that none of the circumstances of Article 276 is present, the Judge may impose the penalty of a fine of one to six months or community service of thirty-one to sixty days.
4.	Anyone who, for agricultural or commercial purposes, without the consent of the holder of a plant variety right and with knowledge of its registration, produces or reproduces, conditions with a view to production or reproduction, offers for sale, sells or otherwise markets, exports or imports, or possesses for any of the aforementioned purposes, plant reproductive or propagating material of a plant variety protected in accordance with national or European Union legislation on the protection of plant varieties, shall be punished with the penalties of one to three years' imprisonment.
Anyone who carries out any of the acts described in the preceding paragraph using, under the denomination of a protected plant variety, plant reproductive or propagating material that does not belong to such variety shall be punished with the same penalty.

Article 275

The same penalties provided for in the preceding article shall be imposed on anyone who intentionally and without being authorised to do so, uses in economic transactions a designation of origin or a geographical indication representing a legally protected specific quality to distinguish the products covered by them, with knowledge of this protection.

Article 276

The penalty of imprisonment of two to six years, a fine of eighteen to thirty-six months and special disqualification from exercising the profession related to the offence committed, for a period of two to five years, shall be imposed when any of the following circumstances is present:
a)	That the benefit obtained or that could have been obtained has special economic significance.
b)	That the facts are especially serious, taking into account the value of the objects illegally produced, distributed, marketed or offered, or the special importance of the damage caused.
c)	That the offender belonged to an organisation or association, even of a temporary nature, whose purpose was to carry out activities infringing industrial property rights.
d)	That persons under 18 years of age are used to commit these offences.

Article 277

Anyone who has intentionally disclosed the invention that is the subject of a secret patent application, in contravention of the provisions of patent legislation, provided that this is to the detriment of national defence, shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of six to twenty-four months.

Section 3 Offences relating to the market and consumers

Article 278

1.	Anyone who, in order to discover a trade secret, seizes by any means data, written or electronic documents, computer media or other objects relating to it, or uses any of the means or instruments referred to in paragraph 1 of Article 197, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment of two to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.
2.	The penalty of imprisonment of three to five years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall be imposed if the secrets discovered are disseminated, revealed or transferred to third parties.
3.	The provisions of this article shall be understood without prejudice to the penalties that may correspond for the seizure or destruction of computer media.

Article 279

The dissemination, revelation or transfer of a trade secret carried out by anyone who has a legal or contractual obligation to maintain confidentiality shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment of two to four years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.
If the secret is used for personal gain, the penalties shall be imposed in their lower half.

Article 280

Anyone who, with knowledge of its illicit origin, and without having taken part in its discovery, carries out any of the conduct described in the two preceding articles, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment of one to three years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

Article 281

1.	Anyone who withdraws from the market raw materials or essential products with the intention of creating a shortage in a sector of the market, forcing a price alteration, or seriously harming consumers, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment of one to five years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.
2.	The penalty in the higher degree shall be imposed if the act is carried out in situations of serious need or catastrophe.

Article 282

Manufacturers or traders who, in their offers or advertising of products or services, make false claims or state untrue characteristics about them, in such a way that they may cause serious and manifest harm to consumers, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment of six months to one year or a fine of 12 to 24 months, without prejudice to the penalty that may apply for the commission of other offences.

Article 282 bis

Those who, as de facto or de jure administrators of a company issuing securities traded on securities markets, falsify the economic-financial information contained in the issue prospectuses of any financial instruments or the information that the company must publish and disseminate in accordance with securities market legislation regarding its resources, activities and present and future business, with the purpose of attracting investors or depositors, placing any type of financial asset, or obtaining financing by any means, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment of one to four years, without prejudice to the provisions of Article 308 of this Code.
In the event that the investment, deposit, placement of the asset or financing is actually obtained, with harm to the investor, depositor, acquirer of the financial assets or creditor, the penalty shall be imposed in the upper half. If the harm caused is of notorious gravity, the penalty to be imposed shall be one to six years' imprisonment and a fine of six to twelve months.

Article 283

Penalties of imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine of six to eighteen months shall be imposed on those who, to the detriment of the consumer, invoice amounts higher than those corresponding to products or services whose cost or price is measured by automatic devices, by means of the alteration or manipulation thereof.

Article 284

1.	The penalty of imprisonment from six months to six years, a fine of two to five years, or from one to three times the benefit obtained or favored, or the losses avoided, if the resulting amount were higher, and special disqualification from intervening in the financial market as an actor, agent or intermediary or informer for a period of two to five years, shall be imposed on those who:
1.	º Using violence, threat, deceit or any other artifice, alter the prices that should result from the free competition of products, goods, financial instruments, spot contracts on raw materials related to them, reference indices, services or any other movable or immovable things that are the object of contracting, without prejudice to the penalty that may correspond to them for other crimes committed.
2.	º By themselves, directly or indirectly or through a means of communication, by means of the internet or through the use of information and communication technologies, or by any other means, disseminate news or rumors or transmit false or misleading signals about persons or companies, knowingly offering economic data that are totally or partially false with the purpose of altering or preserving the listing price of a financial instrument or a spot contract on related raw materials or of manipulating the

calculation of a reference index, when they obtain, for themselves or for a third party, a benefit, provided that any of the following circumstances concur:
a)	that said benefit was greater than two hundred and fifty thousand euros or a loss of identical amount was caused;
b)	that the amount of the funds employed was greater than two million euros;
c)	that a serious impact on the integrity of the market was caused.
3.	º Carry out transactions, transmit false or misleading signals, or give operation orders capable of providing false or misleading indications about the supply, demand or price of a financial instrument, a spot contract on related raw materials or reference indices, or secure, using the same information, by themselves or in concert with others, a dominant position in the market of said instruments or contracts with the purpose of fixing their prices at abnormal or artificial levels, provided that any of the following circumstances concur:
a)	that as a consequence of their conduct they obtain, for themselves or for a third party, a benefit greater than two hundred and fifty thousand euros or cause a loss of identical amount;
b)	that the amount of the funds employed was greater than two million euros;
c)	that a serious impact on the integrity of the market was caused.
2.	The penalty shall be imposed in its upper half if any of the following circumstances concur:
1.	ª That the subject habitually engages in the aforementioned abusive practices.
2.	ª That the benefit obtained, the loss avoided or the damage caused is of notorious importance.
3.	If the person responsible for the act were a worker or employee of an investment services firm, credit institution, supervisory or regulatory authority, or governing entity of regulated markets or trading centers, the penalties shall be imposed in their upper half.

Article 285

1.	Whoever directly or indirectly or through an intermediary carries out acts of acquisition, transmission or transfer of a financial instrument, or of cancellation or modification of an order relating to a financial instrument, using privileged information to which they had reserved access under the terms of paragraph 4, or recommends to a third party the use of said privileged information for any of those acts, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to six years, a fine of two to five years, or from one to three times the benefit obtained or favored or the losses avoided if the resulting amount were higher, and special disqualification from the exercise of the profession or activity for two to five years, provided that any of the following circumstances concur:
a)	that, as a consequence of their conduct they obtain, for themselves or for a third party, a benefit greater than five hundred thousand euros or cause a loss of identical amount;
b)	that the value of the financial instruments employed was greater than two million euros;
c)	that a serious impact on the integrity of the market was caused.
2.	The penalty shall be imposed in its upper half if any of the following circumstances concur:
1.	ª That the subject habitually engages in the aforementioned practices of operations with privileged information.
2.	ª That the benefit obtained, the loss avoided or the damage caused is of notorious importance.
3.	The penalties provided for in this article shall be imposed, in their respective cases, in their upper half if the person responsible for the act were a worker or employee of an investment

services firm, credit institution, supervisory or regulatory authority, or governing entities of regulated markets or trading centers.
4.	For the purposes of this article, it is understood that a person has reserved access to privileged information who is a member of the administration, management or supervisory bodies of the issuer or of the participant in the emissions rights market, who participates in the capital of the issuer or of the participant in the emissions rights market, who knows it on the occasion of the exercise of their professional or business activity, or in the performance of their functions, and who obtains it through a criminal activity.
5.	The same penalties provided for in this article shall be imposed when the person responsible for the act, without having reserved access to the privileged information, obtains it in any manner other than those provided for in the preceding paragraph and uses it knowing that it is privileged information.

Article 285 bis

Outside the cases provided for in the preceding article, whoever possesses privileged information and reveals it outside the normal exercise of their work, profession or functions, endangering the integrity of the market or the confidence of investors, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to four years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification from the exercise of the profession or activity for one to three years.
For the purposes of the provisions of this article, the disclosure of privileged information in a market sounding shall be included when it has been carried out without observing the requirements provided for in European regulations on markets and financial instruments.

Article 285 ter

The provisions of the three preceding articles shall extend to the financial instruments, contracts, conduct, operations and orders provided for in European and Spanish regulations on markets and financial instruments.

Article 285 quater

The incitement, conspiracy and proposal to commit the crimes provided for in articles 284 to 285 bis shall be punished, respectively, with the penalty lower by one or two degrees.

Article 286

1. Anyone who, without the consent of the service provider and for commercial purposes, facilitates intelligible access to a sound or television broadcasting service, to interactive services provided remotely by electronic means, or supplies conditional access to them, considered as an independent service, by means of:
1. The manufacture, importation, distribution, making available by electronic means, sale, rental, or possession of any equipment or computer program, not authorised in another Member State of the European Union, designed or adapted to make such access possible.
2. The installation, maintenance or replacement of the equipment or computer programs mentioned in paragraph 1.
shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from six months to two years and a fine of six to 24 months.
2. Anyone who, with intent to profit, alters or duplicates the identification number of telecommunications equipment, or markets equipment that has undergone fraudulent alteration, shall be punished with an identical penalty.
3. Anyone who, without intent to profit, facilitates to third parties the access described in paragraph 1, or by means of a public communication, commercial or not, supplies information to a plurality of persons about the manner of obtaining unauthorised access to a service or the use of a device or program, of those expressed in that same paragraph 1, inciting to achieve them, shall be imposed the penalty of fine provided therein.

4. Anyone who uses the equipment or programs that permit unauthorised access to conditional access services or telecommunications equipment, shall be imposed the penalty provided in article 255 of this Code regardless of the amount of the fraud.

Section 4 Offences of corruption in business

Article 286 bis

1. The director, administrator, employee or collaborator of a commercial company or a company who, by himself or through an intermediary, receives, requests or accepts an unjustified benefit or advantage of any nature, or an offer or promise to obtain it, for himself or for a third party, as consideration for unduly favouring another in the acquisition or sale of goods, or in the contracting of services or in commercial relations, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to four years, special disqualification for the exercise of industry or commerce for a period of one to six years and a fine of the equivalent to triple the value of the benefit or advantage.
2. Anyone who, by himself or through an intermediary, promises, offers or grants to directors, administrators, employees or collaborators of a commercial company or a company, an unjustified benefit or advantage, of any nature, for them or for third parties, as consideration for unduly favouring him or a third party over others in the acquisition or sale of goods, contracting of services or in commercial relations, shall be punished with the same penalties.
3. The judges and courts, having regard to the amount of the benefit or the value of the advantage, and the importance of the functions of the guilty party, may impose the penalty lower in degree and reduce that of the fine to their prudent discretion.
4. The provisions of this article shall be applicable, in their respective cases, to the directors, administrators, employees or collaborators of a sports entity, whatever its legal form, as well as to sportspersons, referees or judges, with respect to those conducts that have the purpose of predetermining or deliberately and fraudulently altering the result of a test, match or sports competition of special economic or sporting relevance.
For these purposes, a sports competition of special economic relevance shall be considered to be one in which the majority of the participants therein receive any type of remuneration, compensation or economic income for their participation in the activity; and a sports competition of special sporting relevance, one that is classified in the annual sports calendar approved by the corresponding sports federation as an official competition of the highest category of the modality, speciality, or discipline in question.
5. For the purposes of this article, the provisions of article 297 are applicable.

Article 286 ter

1. Those who, by means of the offer, promise or granting of any undue benefit or advantage, pecuniary or of another kind, corrupt or attempt to corrupt, by themselves or through an intermediary, a public authority or official for the benefit of these or of a third party, or attend to their requests in this regard, with the purpose that they act or refrain from acting in relation to the exercise of public functions in order to obtain or retain a contract, business or any other competitive advantage in the conduct of international economic activities, shall be punished, unless they were already punished with a more severe penalty in another provision of this Code, with the penalties of imprisonment from three to six years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, unless the benefit obtained was greater than the resulting amount, in which case the fine shall be the equivalent to triple the amount of said benefit.
In addition to the penalties indicated, the penalty of prohibition from contracting with the public sector shall be imposed in all cases on the person responsible, as well as the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and of the right to enjoy tax and Social Security benefits or incentives, and the prohibition from intervening in commercial transactions of public importance for a period of seven to twelve years.

2. For the purposes of this article, public official shall be understood as those determined by articles 24 and 427.

Article 286 quater

If the acts referred to in the articles of this Section were of special gravity, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half, and may reach the upper degree.
The acts shall be considered, in all cases, of special gravity when:
a) the benefit or advantage has a particularly high value,
b) the action of the perpetrator is not merely occasional,
c) they are acts committed within a criminal organisation or group, or
d) the object of the business concerns humanitarian goods or services or any others of basic necessity.
In the case of paragraph 4 of article 286 bis, the acts shall also be considered of special gravity when:
a) they have the purpose of influencing the development of games of chance or betting; or
b) they are committed in an official sports competition of state scope classified as professional or in an international sports competition.

Section 5 Common provisions to the preceding sections

Article 287

1. To proceed for the offences provided for in Section 3 of this Chapter, except those provided for in articles 284 and 285, a complaint by the aggrieved person or their legal representatives shall be necessary. When the former is a minor, a person with a disability in need of special protection or a helpless person, the Public Prosecutor may also file a complaint.
2. The complaint required in the preceding paragraph shall not be necessary when the commission of the offence affects general interests or a plurality of persons.

Article 288

In the cases provided for in the preceding articles, the publication of the judgment in the official gazettes shall be ordered and, if requested by the injured party, the judge or court may order its total or partial reproduction in any other media outlet, at the expense of the convicted person.
When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is liable for the offences set out in this Chapter, the following penalties shall be imposed:
1. º In the case of the offences provided for in Articles 270, 271, 273, 274, 275, 276, 283 and
286:
a) A fine of double to quadruple the benefit obtained, or that could have been obtained, if the offence committed by the natural person carries a penalty of imprisonment of more than two years.
b) A fine of double to triple the benefit obtained, favoured or that could have been obtained, in all other cases.
2. º In the case of the offences provided for in Articles 277, 278, 279, 280, 281, 282, 282
bis, 284, 285, 285 bis, 285 quater and 286 bis to 286 quater:
a) A fine of two to five years, or triple to quintuple the benefit obtained or that could have been obtained if the resulting amount were higher, when the offence committed by the natural person carries a penalty of more than two years of deprivation of liberty.
b) A fine of six months to two years, or equal to double the benefit obtained or that could have been obtained if the resulting amount were higher, in all other cases.

3. º Having regard to the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

Article 288 bis

In the cases provided for in Articles 281 and 284 of this Code, the directors, de facto or de jure administrators, managers and other current and former members of staff of any company, incorporated or in formation, who in that capacity have committed any of the acts provided for therein, shall be exempt from criminal liability when they put an end to their participation therein and cooperate with the competent authorities in a full, continuous and diligent manner, providing information and evidence which the authorities did not possess, which are useful for the investigation, detection and punishment of the other persons involved, provided that the following conditions are met:
a) They actively cooperate in this regard with the competition authority handling the case,
b) these companies or natural persons have submitted an application for exemption from payment of the fine in accordance with the provisions of the Competition Defence Act,
c) such application has been submitted at a time prior to that at which the directors, de facto or de jure administrators, managers and other current and former members of staff of any company, incorporated or in formation, who in that capacity have been informed that they are being investigated in relation to these acts,
d) it involves active cooperation also with the judicial authority or the Public Prosecutor's Office by providing useful and concrete indications to secure proof of the offence and identify other perpetrators.

CHAPTER XII

On the removal of one's own property from its social or cultural utility

Article 289

Whoever by any means destroys, renders useless or damages his own property of social or cultural utility, or in any way removes it from compliance with legal duties imposed in the interest of the community, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of three to five months or a fine of six to 10 months.

CHAPTER XIII

On corporate offences

Article 290

The de facto or de jure administrators of a company incorporated or in formation, who falsify the annual accounts or other documents that must reflect the legal or economic situation of the entity, in a manner capable of causing economic harm to it, to any of its partners, or to a third party, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of one to three years and a fine of six to twelve months.
If the economic harm is actually caused, the penalties shall be imposed in their upper half.

Article 291

Those who, taking advantage of their majority position in the shareholders' meeting or the management body of any company incorporated or in formation, impose abusive resolutions, with intent to profit themselves or others, to the detriment of the other partners, and without

bringing benefits to the company, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of six months to three years or a fine of equal to triple the benefit obtained.

Article 292

The same penalty as in the preceding article shall be imposed on those who impose or take advantage for themselves or for a third party, to the detriment of the company or any of its partners, of a harmful resolution adopted by a fictitious majority, obtained by abuse of signature in blank, by undue attribution of voting rights to those who legally lack them, by unlawful denial of the exercise of this right to those who have it recognised by law, or by any other similar means or procedure, and without prejudice to punishing the act as appropriate if it constitutes another offence.

Article 293

The de facto or de jure administrators of any company incorporated or in formation, who without legal cause deny or prevent a partner from exercising the rights of information, participation in management or control of corporate activity, or preferential subscription of shares recognised by law, shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months.

Article 294

Those who, as de facto or de jure administrators of any company incorporated or in formation, subject to or acting in markets subject to administrative supervision, deny or prevent the action of inspecting or supervising persons, bodies or entities, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of six months to three years or a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.
In addition to the penalties provided for in the preceding paragraph, the judicial authority may order some of the measures provided for in Article 129 of this Code.

Article 295. (Deleted)

Article 296

1. The acts described in this chapter may only be prosecuted upon complaint by the aggrieved person or his legal representative. When the latter is a minor, a person with a disability in need of special protection or a helpless person, the Public Prosecutor's Office may also file a complaint.
2. The complaint required in the preceding paragraph shall not be necessary when the commission of the offence affects general interests or a plurality of persons.

Article 297

For the purposes of this chapter, a company is understood to mean any cooperative, savings bank, mutual society, financial or credit institution, foundation, commercial company or any other entity of a similar nature which, in order to fulfil its purposes, participates permanently in the market.

CHAPTER XIV

On receiving and money laundering

Article 298

1.	Whoever, with intent to profit and with knowledge of the commission of a crime against property or the socio-economic order, in which he has not participated either as perpetrator or as accomplice, assists those responsible in taking advantage of the proceeds thereof, or receives, acquires or conceals such proceeds, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to two years.

A sentence of one to three years' imprisonment shall be imposed in the following cases:
a)	When the items are of artistic, historical, cultural or scientific value.
b)	When the items are essential goods, conduits, cabling, equipment or components of electrical supply infrastructures or telecommunications services, or other items intended for the provision of services of general interest, agricultural or livestock products or the instruments or means used to obtain them.
c)	When the acts are particularly serious, having regard to the value of the proceeds received or the damage that their removal would foreseeably have caused.
2.	These penalties shall be imposed in their upper half on whoever receives, acquires or conceals the proceeds of the crime in order to traffic in them. If the trafficking is carried out using a commercial or industrial establishment or premises, the penalty of a fine of twelve to twenty-four months shall also be imposed. In these cases the judges or courts, having regard to the seriousness of the act and the personal circumstances of the offender, may also impose on him the penalty of special disqualification from practising his profession or industry for a period of two to five years and order the temporary or permanent closure of the establishment or premises. If the closure is temporary, its duration may not exceed five years.
3.	In no case may a custodial sentence exceeding that prescribed for the concealed crime be imposed. If the latter is punishable by a penalty of another nature, the custodial sentence shall be replaced by a fine of 12 to 24 months, unless the concealed crime carries an equal or lesser penalty; in such case, the offender shall be sentenced to the penalty for that crime in its lower half.

Article 299. (Deleted)

Article 300

The provisions of this chapter shall apply even when the perpetrator or accomplice of the act from which the proceeds taken advantage of originated was not responsible or was personally exempt from punishment.

Article 301

1.	Whoever acquires, possesses, uses, converts or transmits property, knowing that it originates from criminal activity, committed by him or by any third person, or performs any other act to conceal or cover up its illicit origin, or to assist the person who has participated in the offence or offences to evade the legal consequences of his acts, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to six years and a fine of one to three times the value of the property. In these cases, the judges or courts, having regard to the seriousness of the act and the personal circumstances of the offender, may also impose on him the penalty of special disqualification from practising his profession or industry for a period of one to three years, and order the temporary or permanent closure of the establishment or premises. If the closure is temporary, its duration may not exceed five years.
The penalty shall be imposed in its upper half when the property originates from any of the crimes related to trafficking in toxic drugs, narcotics or psychotropic substances described in articles 368 to 372 of this Code. In these cases the provisions contained in article 374 of this Code shall apply.
The penalty shall also be imposed in its upper half when the property originates from any of the crimes included in title VII bis, chapter V of title VIII, section 4 of chapter XI of title XIII, title XV bis, chapter I of title XVI or chapters V, VI, VII, VIII, IX and X of title XIX.
2.	The same penalties shall be imposed, as the case may be, for the concealment or covering up of the true nature, origin, location, destination, movement or rights over the property or ownership thereof, knowing that they proceed from any of the crimes referred to in the preceding paragraph or from an act of participation in them.

3.	If the acts are committed through gross negligence, the penalty shall be imprisonment of six months to two years and a fine of one to three times the amount.
4.	The offender shall also be punished even if the crime from which the property originated, or the acts punished in the preceding paragraphs, had been committed, wholly or partly, abroad.
5.	If the offender has obtained profits, they shall be confiscated in accordance with the rules of article 127 of this Code.

Article 302

1.	In the cases provided for in the preceding article, custodial sentences shall be imposed in their upper half on persons who belong to an organisation dedicated to the purposes indicated therein, and the penalty one degree higher on the leaders, administrators or managers of the said organisations.
The penalty shall also be imposed in its upper half on those who, being obliged subjects under the regulations on the prevention of money laundering and terrorist financing, commit any of the conducts described in article 301 in the exercise of their professional activity.
2.	In such cases, when in accordance with the provisions of article 31 bis a legal person is liable, the following penalties shall be imposed:
a)	A fine of two to five years, if the crime committed by the natural person carries a prison sentence of more than five years.
b)	A fine of six months to two years, in all other cases.
Having regard to the rules established in article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of article 33.

Article 303

If the acts provided for in the preceding articles are committed by a businessman, intermediary in the financial sector, medical practitioner, public official, social worker, teacher or educator, in the exercise of his position, profession or occupation, he shall be sentenced, in addition to the corresponding penalty, to special disqualification from public employment or office, profession or occupation, industry or trade, for three to ten years. The penalty of absolute disqualification for ten to twenty years shall be imposed when the said acts are committed by an authority or agent thereof.
For this purpose, medical practitioners are understood to be doctors, psychologists, persons holding health qualifications, veterinarians, pharmacists and their employees.

Article 304

Incitement, conspiracy and proposal to commit the crimes provided for in articles 301 to 303 shall be punished, respectively, with the penalty one or two degrees lower.

TITLE XIII BIS

Crimes of illegal financing of political parties.

Article 304 bis

1.	Whoever receives donations or contributions intended for a political party, federation, coalition or grouping of electors in violation of the provisions of Article 5.One of Organic Law 8/2007, of 4 July, on the financing of political parties, shall be punished with a fine of three to five times their value.
2.	The foregoing acts shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to four years and a fine of three to five times their value or the excess when:
a)	They involve donations covered by Article 5.One, letters a) or c) of Organic Law 8/2007, of 4 July, on the financing of political parties, in an amount

exceeding 500,000 euros, or which exceed by this amount the limit set in letter b) of that provision, when it is the latter that has been infringed.
b)	They involve donations covered by Article 7.Two of Organic Law 8/2007, of 4 July, on the financing of political parties, which exceed the amount of 100,000 euros.
3.	If the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph are of particular gravity, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half, and may reach the next higher degree.
4.	The same penalties shall be imposed, in their respective cases, on whoever delivers donations or contributions intended for a political party, federation, coalition or grouping of electors, directly or through an intermediary, in any of the cases referred to in the preceding paragraphs.
5.	The same penalties shall be imposed when, in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis of this Code, a legal person is responsible for the acts. Having regard to the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

Article 304 ter

1.	Whoever participates in structures or organisations, whatever their nature, whose purpose is the financing of political parties, federations, coalitions or groupings of electors, outside what is established by law, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one to five years.
2.	The penalty shall be imposed in its upper half on persons who direct such structures or organisations.
3.	If the acts referred to in the preceding paragraphs are of particular gravity, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half, and may reach the next higher degree.

TITLE XIV

Offences against the Public Treasury and against Social Security

Article 305

1. Whoever, by action or omission, defrauds the state, autonomous, foral or local Public Treasury, evading the payment of taxes, amounts withheld or that should have been withheld or payments on account, unduly obtaining refunds or enjoying tax benefits in the same manner, provided that the amount of the defrauded quota, the amount not paid of the withholdings or payments on account or of the refunds or tax benefits unduly obtained or enjoyed exceeds one hundred and twenty thousand euros shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years and a fine of one to six times the aforementioned amount, unless he had regularised his tax situation in the terms of paragraph 4 of this article.
The mere filing of declarations or self-assessments does not exclude fraud, when this is proven by other facts.
In addition to the penalties indicated, the person responsible shall be imposed the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and of the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of three to six years.
2. For the purposes of determining the amount mentioned in the preceding paragraph:
a) If it concerns taxes, withholdings, payments on account or refunds, periodic or of periodic declaration, reference shall be made to what was defrauded in each tax or declaration period, and if these are less than twelve months, the amount defrauded shall refer to the calendar year. Notwithstanding the foregoing, in cases where the fraud is carried out within a criminal organisation or group, or by persons or entities acting under the appearance of a real economic activity without effectively carrying it out, the offence shall be prosecutable from the moment the amount set in paragraph 1 is reached.

b) In other cases, the amount shall be understood to refer to each of the different concepts for which a taxable event is susceptible to assessment.
3. The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever commits the conduct described in paragraph 1 and on whoever evades the payment of any amount that must be paid or unduly enjoys a benefit obtained legally, when the acts are committed against the Treasury of the European Union, provided that the defrauded amount exceeded one hundred thousand euros within a calendar year. Notwithstanding the foregoing, in cases where the fraud is carried out within a criminal organisation or group, or by persons or entities acting under the appearance of a real economic activity without effectively carrying it out, the offence shall be prosecutable from the moment the amount set in this paragraph is reached.
If the defrauded amount does not exceed one hundred thousand euros but exceeds ten thousand, a prison sentence of three months to one year or a fine of one to three times the aforementioned amount and the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and of the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of six months to two years shall be imposed.
4. The tax situation shall be considered regularised when the taxpayer has proceeded to the complete recognition and payment of the tax debt, before the Tax Administration has notified him of the commencement of verification or investigation actions aimed at determining the tax debts subject to regularisation or, in the event that such actions have not occurred, before the Public Prosecutor, the State Attorney or the procedural representative of the autonomous, foral or local Administration concerned, files a complaint or report against him, or before the Public Prosecutor or the Investigating Judge carry out actions that allow him to have formal knowledge of the initiation of proceedings.
Likewise, the effects of the regularisation provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be applicable when tax debts are satisfied once the Administration's right to determine them through administrative channels has prescribed.
The regularisation by the taxpayer of his tax situation shall prevent him from being prosecuted for possible accounting irregularities or other instrumental falsehoods that, exclusively in relation to the tax debt subject to regularisation, he may have committed prior to the regularisation of his tax situation.
5. When the Tax Administration appreciates indications that an offence against the Public Treasury has been committed, it may assess separately, on the one hand the concepts and amounts that are not linked to the possible offence against the Public Treasury, and on the other, those that are linked to the possible offence against the Public Treasury.
The assessment indicated first in the preceding paragraph shall follow the ordinary procedure and shall be subject to the appeals regime applicable to any tax assessment. And the assessment that may derive from those concepts and amounts that are linked to the possible offence against the Public Treasury shall follow the procedure established for that purpose by tax regulations, without prejudice to it finally being adjusted to what is decided in the criminal proceedings.
The existence of criminal proceedings for an offence against the Public Treasury shall not paralyse the collection action of the tax debt. The Tax Administration may initiate actions aimed at collection, unless the Judge, ex officio or at the request of a party, has agreed to suspend the enforcement actions, upon provision of security. If security cannot be provided in whole or in part, exceptionally the Judge may agree to the suspension with total or partial waiver of security if he considers that the enforcement could cause irreparable damage or damage that is very difficult to repair.
6. Judges and Courts may impose on the taxpayer or the perpetrator of the offence a penalty lower by one or two degrees, provided that, before two months elapse from the judicial summons as a defendant, he satisfies the tax debt and judicially acknowledges the facts. The foregoing shall be equally applicable with respect to other participants in the offence other than the taxpayer or the perpetrator of the offence, when they actively collaborate to obtain decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other persons responsible, for the complete clarification of the criminal facts or for the discovery of the assets of the taxpayer or other persons responsible for the offence.

7. In proceedings for the offence contemplated in this article, for the execution of the fine penalty and civil liability, which shall comprise the amount of the tax debt that the Tax Administration has not assessed due to prescription or other legal cause in the terms provided for in Law 58/2003, General Tax Law, of 17 December, including its late payment interest, Judges and Courts shall request the assistance of the services of the Tax Administration which shall demand it through the administrative enforcement procedure in the terms established in the aforementioned Law.

Artículo 305 bis

1.	The offence against the Public Treasury shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to six years and a fine of double to six times the defrauded amount when the fraud is committed with any of the following circumstances:
a)	That the amount of the defrauded amount exceeds six hundred thousand euros.
b)	That the fraud has been committed within a criminal organisation or group.
c)	That the use of interposed natural or legal persons or entities without legal personality, fiduciary businesses or instruments or tax havens or territories with zero taxation conceals or hinders the determination of the identity of the taxpayer or the person responsible for the offence, the determination of the defrauded amount or the assets of the taxpayer or the person responsible for the offence.
2.	All other provisions contained in Article 305 shall apply to the cases described in this article.
In these cases, in addition to the penalties indicated, the person responsible shall be subject to the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of four to eight years.

Artículo 306

Whoever by action or omission defrauds the general budgets of the European Union or others administered by it, in an amount exceeding fifty thousand euros, evading, outside the cases contemplated in paragraph 3 of Article 305, the payment of amounts that should be paid or, giving, outside the cases contemplated in Article 308, the funds obtained an application different from that for which they were intended or unduly obtaining funds by falsifying the conditions required for their granting or concealing those that would have prevented it, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years and a fine of the same amount to six times the said amount and the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of three to six years.
If the amount defrauded or unduly applied does not exceed fifty thousand euros, but exceeds four thousand, a prison sentence of three months to one year or a fine of the same amount to three times the said amount and the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of six months to two years shall be imposed.

Artículo 307

1.	Whoever, by action or omission, defrauds the Social Security by evading the payment of its contributions and jointly collected items, unduly obtaining refunds thereof or enjoying deductions for any item also unduly, provided that the amount of the defrauded contributions or undue refunds or deductions exceeds fifty thousand euros, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years and a fine of the same amount to six times the said amount unless he has regularised his situation before the Social Security in the terms of paragraph 3 of this article.
The mere submission of contribution documents does not exclude fraud, when it is proven by other facts.

In addition to the penalties indicated, the person responsible shall be subject to the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of three to six years.
2.	For the purposes of determining the amount mentioned in the preceding paragraph, reference shall be made to the total amount defrauded during four calendar years.
3.	The situation before the Social Security shall be considered regularised when the person obliged to the Social Security has proceeded to the complete acknowledgement and payment of the debt before being notified of the initiation of inspection proceedings aimed at determining such debts or, in the event that such proceedings have not taken place, before the Public Prosecutor or the Social Security Lawyer files a complaint or denunciation against him or before the Public Prosecutor or the Investigating Judge carries out actions that allow him to have formal knowledge of the initiation of proceedings.
Likewise, the effects of the regularisation provided for in the preceding paragraph shall apply when debts to the Social Security are satisfied once the Administration's right to determine them through administrative channels has prescribed.
The regularisation of the situation before the Social Security shall prevent such person from being prosecuted for possible accounting irregularities or other instrumental falsifications that, exclusively in relation to the debt subject to regularisation, he may have committed prior to the regularisation of his situation.
4.	The existence of criminal proceedings for an offence against the Social Security shall not suspend the administrative procedure for the settlement and collection of the debt contracted with the Social Security, unless the Judge so orders upon provision of a guarantee. In the event that a guarantee cannot be provided in whole or in part, the Judge, exceptionally, may order the suspension with total or partial waiver of guarantees, in the event that he considers that enforcement could cause irreparable damage or damage that is very difficult to repair. The administrative settlement shall ultimately be adjusted to what is decided in the criminal proceedings.
5.	Judges and Courts may impose on the person obliged to the Social Security or the perpetrator of the offence a penalty one or two degrees lower, provided that, before two months have elapsed from the judicial summons as a defendant, he satisfies the debt with the Social Security and judicially acknowledges the facts. The foregoing shall also apply with respect to other participants in the offence other than the debtor to the Social Security or the perpetrator of the offence, when they actively collaborate in obtaining decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other persons responsible, for the complete clarification of the criminal facts or for the discovery of the assets of the person obliged to the Social Security or other persons responsible for the offence.
6.	In proceedings for the offence contemplated in this article, for the enforcement of the fine and civil liability, which shall include the amount of the debt to the Social Security that the Administration has not settled due to prescription or other legal cause, including interest for late payment, Judges and Courts shall request the assistance of the services of the Social Security Administration, which shall demand it through the administrative enforcement procedure.

Article 307 bis

1.	The offence against Social Security shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to six years and a fine of double to six times the amount when any of the following circumstances are present in the commission of the offence:
a)	That the amount of contributions defrauded or of undue refunds or deductions exceeds one hundred and twenty thousand euros.
b)	That the fraud has been committed within a criminal organisation or group.
c)	That the use of interposed natural or legal persons or entities without legal personality, fiduciary businesses or instruments or tax havens or territories with zero taxation conceals or hinders the determination of the identity of the person obliged to Social Security or of the person responsible for the offence, the determination of the amount defrauded or of the assets of the person obliged to Social Security or of the person responsible for the offence.

2.	All other provisions contained in Article 307 shall apply to the cases described in this article.
3.	In these cases, in addition to the penalties indicated, the person responsible shall be subject to the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and of the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of four to eight years.

Article 307 ter

1.	Whoever obtains, for himself or for another, the enjoyment of benefits from the Social Security System, the undue prolongation thereof, or facilitates their obtaining by others, by means of error provoked through the simulation or misrepresentation of facts, or the conscious concealment of facts which he had a duty to report, thereby causing harm to the Public Administration, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years.
When the acts, in view of the amount defrauded, the means employed and the personal circumstances of the perpetrator, are not of particular gravity, they shall be punished with a fine of one to six times the amount.
In addition to the penalties indicated, the person responsible shall be subject to the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies and of the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of three to six years.
2.	When the value of the benefits exceeds fifty thousand euros or any of the circumstances referred to in letters b) or c) of paragraph 1 of Article 307 bis is present, a prison sentence of two to six years and a fine of one to six times the amount shall be imposed.
In these cases, in addition to the penalties indicated, the person responsible shall be subject to the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies and of the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of four to eight years.
3.	The person who reimburses an amount equivalent to the value of the benefit received increased by an annual interest equivalent to the legal interest rate on money increased by two percentage points, from the moment he received it, before he has been notified of the initiation of inspection and control proceedings in relation thereto or, in the event that such proceedings have not taken place, before the Public Prosecutor, the State Attorney, the Social Security Lawyer, or the representative of the relevant regional or local Administration, files a complaint or report against him or before the Public Prosecutor or the Investigating Judge carries out actions that enable him to have formal knowledge of the initiation of proceedings, shall be exempt from criminal liability in relation to the conduct described in the preceding paragraphs.
The exemption from criminal liability contemplated in the preceding paragraph shall also extend to said person for any instrumental falsifications that, exclusively in relation to the defrauded benefits subject to reimbursement, he may have committed prior to the regularisation of his situation.
4.	The existence of criminal proceedings for any of the offences in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this article shall not prevent the competent Administration from demanding reimbursement by administrative means of benefits unduly obtained. The amount to be reimbursed shall be deemed provisionally fixed by the Administration, and shall be adjusted afterwards to what is finally decided in the criminal proceedings.
The criminal proceedings shall not paralyse the collection action of the competent Administration either, which may initiate actions directed at collection unless the Judge, ex officio or at the request of a party, has ordered the suspension of enforcement proceedings upon provision of security. If security cannot be provided in whole or in part, exceptionally the Judge may order suspension with total or partial waiver of security if he considers that enforcement could cause irreparable harm or harm that is very difficult to repair.
5.	In proceedings for the offence contemplated in this article, for the enforcement of the fine and civil liability, the Judges and Courts shall request the assistance of the services of the Social Security Administration which shall demand them by the administrative enforcement procedure.

6.	The provisions of paragraph 5 of Article 307 of the Criminal Code shall apply to the cases regulated in this article.

Article 308

1.	Whoever obtains subsidies or aid from Public Administrations, including the European Union, in an amount or value exceeding one hundred thousand euros by falsifying the conditions required for their granting or concealing those that would have prevented it shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years and a fine of one to six times its amount, unless he or she carries out the reimbursement referred to in paragraph 6.
2.	The same penalties shall be imposed on whoever, in the development of an activity financed totally or partially with funds from Public Administrations, including the European Union, applies them in an amount exceeding one hundred thousand euros to purposes other than those for which the subsidy or aid was granted, unless he or she carries out the reimbursement referred to in paragraph 6.
3.	In addition to the penalties indicated, the person responsible shall be subject to the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and of the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of three to six years.
4.	If the amount obtained, defrauded or improperly applied does not exceed one hundred thousand euros but exceeds ten thousand, a prison sentence of three months to one year or a fine of one to three times the said amount shall be imposed, and the loss of the possibility of obtaining subsidies or public aid and of the right to enjoy tax or Social Security benefits or incentives for a period of six months to two years, unless he or she carries out the reimbursement referred to in paragraph 6.
5.	For the purposes of determining the amount referred to in this article, the total amount obtained, defrauded or improperly applied shall be taken into account, regardless of whether it comes from one or several Public Administrations jointly.
6.	The reimbursement referred to in paragraphs 1, 2 and 4 shall be deemed to have been made when the recipient of the subsidy or aid proceeds to return the subsidies or aid improperly received or applied, increased by the default interest applicable in subsidy matters from the moment they were received, and it is carried out before notification of the initiation of verification or control actions in relation to said subsidies or aid or, in the event that such actions have not taken place, before the Public Prosecutor, the State Attorney or the representative of the autonomous or local Administration concerned files a criminal complaint or report against him or her or before the Public Prosecutor or the investigating judge carries out actions that allow him or her to have formal knowledge of the initiation of proceedings. The reimbursement shall prevent said subject from being prosecuted for possible instrumental falsifications that, exclusively in relation to the debt subject to regularisation, he or she may have committed prior to the regularisation of his or her situation.
7.	The existence of criminal proceedings for any of the offences in paragraphs 1, 2 and 4 of this article shall not prevent the competent Administration from requiring reimbursement by administrative means of the subsidies or aid improperly applied.
