The authority or public official who, lacking the powers to do so, issues a general provision or suspends its execution, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of one to three years, a fine of six to twelve months and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of six to twelve years.

Article 506

bis. (Deleted)

Article 507

The Judge or Magistrate who arrogates to himself administrative powers which he lacks, or prevents their lawful exercise by the person who holds them, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of six months to one year, a fine of three to six months and suspension from public employment or office for a period of one to three years.

Article 508

1. The authority or public official who arrogates to himself judicial powers or prevents the execution of a decision issued by the competent judicial authority shall be punished with sentences of imprisonment of six months to one year, a fine of three to eight months and suspension from public employment or office for a period of one to three years.
2. The administrative or military authority or official who attacks the independence of Judges or Magistrates, guaranteed by the Constitution, by addressing to them instructions, orders or intimidation relating to cases or proceedings of which they have knowledge, shall be punished with a sentence of imprisonment of one to two years, a fine of four to ten months and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of two to six years.

Article 509

The Judge or Magistrate, the authority or the public official who, legally required to decline jurisdiction, continues to proceed without waiting for the corresponding jurisdictional conflict to be decided, except in cases permitted by Law, shall be punished with a fine of three to ten months and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of six months to one year.

CHAPTER IV

Offences relating to the exercise of fundamental rights and public freedoms.

Section 1

Offences committed on the occasion of the exercise of fundamental rights and public freedoms guaranteed by the Constitution

Article 510

1.	The following shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to four years and a fine of six to twelve months:
a)	Those who publicly foment, promote or directly or indirectly incite hatred, hostility, discrimination or violence against a group, a part thereof or against a specific person by reason of their membership therein, on racist, anti-Semitic, anti-Gypsy or other grounds relating to ideology, religion or beliefs, family situation, the membership of its members in an ethnic group, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, sexual orientation or identity, on grounds of gender, aporophobia, illness or disability.
b)	Those who produce, prepare, possess for the purpose of distribution, provide third parties with access to, distribute, disseminate or sell writings or any other kind of material or media which by their content are suitable for fomenting, promoting, or directly or indirectly inciting hatred, hostility, discrimination or violence against a group, a part thereof, or against a specific person by reason of their membership therein, on racist, anti-Semitic, anti-Gypsy or other grounds relating to ideology, religion or beliefs, family situation, the membership of its members in an ethnic group, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, sexual orientation or identity, on grounds of gender, aporophobia, illness or disability.
c)	Those who publicly deny, seriously trivialise or glorify the crimes of genocide, crimes against humanity or against persons and property protected in the event of armed conflict, or glorify their perpetrators, when they have been committed against a group or a part thereof, or against a specific person by reason of their membership therein, on racist, anti-Semitic, anti-Gypsy, or other grounds relating to ideology, religion or beliefs, family situation or the membership of its members in an ethnic group, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, sexual orientation or identity, on grounds of gender, aporophobia, illness or disability, when in this way a climate of violence, hostility, hatred or discrimination against them is promoted or favoured.
2.	The following shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to two years and a fine of six to twelve months:
a)	Those who injure the dignity of persons through actions that entail humiliation, contempt or discredit of any of the groups referred to in the preceding paragraph, or of a part thereof, or of any specific person by reason of their membership therein on racist, anti-Semitic, anti-Gypsy or other grounds relating to ideology, religion or beliefs, family situation, the membership of its members in an ethnic group, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, sexual orientation or identity, on grounds of gender, aporophobia, illness or disability, or produce, prepare, possess for the purpose of distribution, provide third parties with access to, distribute, disseminate or sell writings or any other kind of material or media which by their content are suitable for injuring the dignity of persons by representing a serious humiliation, contempt or discredit of any of the groups mentioned, of a part thereof, or of any specific person by reason of their membership therein.
b)	Those who glorify or justify by any means of public expression or dissemination the crimes that have been committed against a group, a part thereof, or against a specific person by reason of their membership therein on racist, anti-Semitic, anti-Gypsy or other grounds relating to ideology, religion or beliefs, family situation, the membership of its members in an ethnic group, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, sexual orientation or identity, on grounds of gender, aporophobia, illness or disability, or those who have participated in their execution.
The acts shall be punished with a sentence of one to four years' imprisonment and a fine of six to twelve months when in this way a climate of violence, hostility, hatred or discrimination against the aforementioned groups is promoted or favoured.

3.	The penalties provided for in the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed in their upper half when the acts have been carried out through a social media outlet, by means of the internet or through the use of information technologies, in such a way that it became accessible to a large number of persons.
4.	When the acts, in view of their circumstances, are suitable for disturbing public peace or creating a serious feeling of insecurity or fear among the members of the group, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half, which may be increased up to the next degree.
5.	In all cases, the penalty of special disqualification for educational professions or occupations, in the teaching, sports and leisure fields, shall also be imposed for a period between three and ten years longer than the duration of the custodial sentence imposed, if applicable, in the judgment, taking proportionate account of the seriousness of the crime, the number of those committed and the circumstances present in the offender.
6.	The judge or court shall order the destruction, erasure or rendering unusable of books, files, documents, articles and any kind of media that are the object of the crime referred to in the preceding paragraphs or by means of which it has been committed. When the crime has been committed through information and communication technologies, the removal of the contents shall be ordered.
In cases where, through an internet access portal or information society service, the contents referred to in the preceding paragraph are disseminated exclusively or predominantly, the blocking of access or the interruption of the provision thereof shall be ordered.

Article 510 bis

When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is liable for the crimes included in the two preceding articles, it shall be imposed the penalty of a fine of two to five years. Having regard to the rules established in Article 66 bis, judges and courts may also impose the penalties set out in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.
In this case, the provisions of number 3 of Article 510 of the Criminal Code shall also be applicable.

Artículo 511

1.	A private individual in charge of a public service who denies a person a benefit to which they are entitled by reason of their ideology, religion or beliefs, their family situation, belonging to an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, age, sexual orientation or gender identity, gender reasons, aporophobia or social exclusion, the illness they suffer from or their disability, shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of one to three years.
2.	The same penalties shall apply when the acts are committed against an association, foundation, society or corporation or against its members by reason of their ideology, religion or beliefs, their family situation, the belonging of its members or any of them to an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, age, sexual orientation or gender identity, gender reasons, aporophobia or social exclusion, the illness they suffer from or their disability.
3.	Public officials who commit any of the acts provided for in this article shall incur the same penalties in their upper half and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of two to four years.
4.	In all cases, the penalty of special disqualification for educational profession or occupation, in the teaching, sports and leisure fields, shall also be imposed for a period of between one and three years longer than the duration of the penalty imposed if it were one of deprivation of liberty; when the penalty imposed is a fine, the penalty of special disqualification shall have a duration of one to three years. In all cases, the seriousness of the offence and the circumstances present in the offender shall be taken into account proportionally.

Artículo 512

Those who in the exercise of their professional or business activities deny a person a benefit to which they are entitled by reason of their ideology, religion or beliefs, their family situation, their belonging to an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, age, sexual orientation or gender identity, gender reasons, aporophobia or social exclusion, the illness they suffer from or their disability, shall incur the penalty of special disqualification for the exercise of profession, occupation, industry or trade and special disqualification for educational profession or occupation, in the teaching, sports and leisure fields for a period of one to four years.

Artículo 513

Unlawful meetings or demonstrations are punishable, and the following are considered as such:
1.	º Those held for the purpose of committing an offence.
2.	º Those attended by persons with weapons, explosive devices or blunt objects or in any other dangerous manner.

Artículo 514

1.	The promoters or leaders of any meeting or demonstration covered by number 1.º of the preceding article and those who, in relation to number 2.º thereof, have not tried to prevent by all means at their disposal the circumstances mentioned therein, shall incur the penalties of imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months. For these purposes, the leaders or promoters of the meeting or demonstration shall be deemed to be those who convene or preside over them.
2.	Those attending a meeting or demonstration who carry weapons or other equally dangerous means shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from one to two years and a fine of six to twelve months. Judges or Courts, taking into account the subject's background, circumstances of the case and characteristics of the weapon or instrument carried, may reduce the prescribed penalty by one degree.
3.	Persons who, on the occasion of the holding of a meeting or demonstration, commit acts of violence against the authority, its agents, persons or public or private property, shall be punished with the penalty corresponding to their offence, in its upper half.
4.	Those who prevent the legitimate exercise of the freedoms of assembly or demonstration, or seriously disturb the conduct of a lawful meeting or demonstration shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from two to three years if the acts are carried out with violence, and with the penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or a fine of six to 12 months if they are committed by means of assault or any other unlawful procedure.
5.	The promoters or leaders of any meeting or demonstration who convene, hold or attempt to hold again a meeting or demonstration that has been previously suspended or prohibited, and provided that they thereby intend to subvert the constitutional order or seriously disturb the public peace, shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from six months to one year and a fine of six to twelve months, without prejudice to the penalty that may correspond, where appropriate, in accordance with the preceding paragraphs.

Artículo 515

Unlawful associations are punishable, the following being considered as such:
1.	º Those whose purpose is to commit an offence or, after being constituted, promote its commission.
2.	º Those which, even having a lawful purpose, employ violent means or means of alteration or control of personality to achieve it.
3.	º Organisations of a paramilitary nature.
4.	º Those which encourage, promote or incite directly or indirectly hatred, hostility, discrimination or violence against persons, groups or associations by reason of their ideology, religion or beliefs, the belonging of their members or any of them to an ethnicity, race or nation, their national origin, their sex, age, sexual orientation or gender identity,

gender reasons, aporophobia or social exclusion, family situation, illness or disability.

Artículo 516. (Suprimido)

Artículo 517

In the cases provided for in numbers 1.º and 3.º to 6.º of article 515 (*) the following penalties shall be imposed:
1.	º On the founders, directors and presidents of the associations, imprisonment from two to four years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months and special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of six to twelve years.
2.	º On active members, imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months.

(*) La remisión a los números 1º y 3º al 6º del art. 515 se entiende hecha a los actuales números 1º a 4º del art. 515, tras la modificación efectuada por el art. único.239 de la L.O. 1/2015, de 30 de marzo. Ref. BOE-A-2015-3439.

Artículo 518

Those who with their economic cooperation or of any other kind, in any case relevant, favour the foundation, organisation or activity of the associations covered by numbers 1.o and 3.o to 6.o of article 515(*), shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from one to three years, a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, and disqualification from public employment or office for a period of one to four years.

(*) La remisión a los números 1º y 3º al 6º del art. 515 se entiende hecha a los actuales números 1º a 4º del art. 515, tras la modificación efectuada por el art. único.239 de la L.O. 1/2015, de 30 de marzo. Ref. BOE-A-2015-3439.

Artículo 519

Provocation, conspiracy and proposal to commit the offence of unlawful association shall be punished with a penalty one or two degrees lower than that corresponding, respectively, to the acts provided for in the preceding articles.

Artículo 520

Judges or Courts, in the cases provided for in article 515, shall order the dissolution of the unlawful association and, where appropriate, any other of the ancillary consequences of article 129 of this Code.

Artículo 521

In the offence of unlawful association, if the offender is an authority, agent thereof or public official, they shall be imposed, in addition to the prescribed penalties, that of absolute disqualification from ten to fifteen years.

Artículo 521

bis. (Suprimido)

Section 2

Offences against freedom of conscience, religious feelings and respect for the deceased

Article 522

The following shall incur a fine of four to ten months:

1. Those who by means of violence, intimidation, force or any other unlawful coercion prevent a member or members of a religious denomination from practising the acts proper to the beliefs they profess, or from attending them.
2. Those who by the same means force another or others to practise or attend acts of worship or rites, or to perform acts revealing that they profess or do not profess a religion, or to change the one they profess.

Article 523

Whoever by violence, threat, tumult or acts of force prevents, interrupts or disturbs the acts, functions, ceremonies or manifestations of religious denominations registered in the corresponding public register of the Ministry of Justice and the Interior, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to six years, if the act has been committed in a place intended for worship, and with a fine of four to ten months if it is carried out in any other place.

Article 524

Whoever in a temple, place intended for worship or in religious ceremonies performs acts of desecration in offence of legally protected religious feelings shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to one year or a fine of 12 to 24 months.

Article 525

1. Those who, in order to offend the feelings of the members of a religious denomination, publicly mock, by word, in writing or by means of any type of document, their dogmas, beliefs, rites or ceremonies, or publicly vex those who profess or practise them, shall incur a fine of eight to twelve months.
2. The same penalties shall be incurred by those who publicly mock, by word or in writing, those who profess no religion or belief whatsoever.

Article 526

Whoever, failing in the respect due to the memory of the dead, violates sepulchres or graves, desecrates a corpse or its ashes or, with intent to outrage, destroys, alters or damages funerary urns, pantheons, gravestones or niches shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from three to five months or a fine of six to 10 months.

Article 527

(Without content)

Article 528. (Repealed)

CHAPTER V

Offences committed by public officials against constitutional guarantees

Section 1

Offences committed by public officials against individual liberty

Article 529

1. The Judge or Magistrate who delivers a criminal case to another authority or official, military or administrative, who unlawfully demands it, shall be punished with a penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of six months to two years.
2. If he also delivers the person of a detainee, the penalty immediately above in degree shall be imposed.

Article 530

The authority or public official who, in a case involving an offence, orders, carries out or prolongs any deprivation of liberty of a detainee, prisoner or convicted person, in violation of the time limits or other constitutional or legal guarantees, shall be punished with a penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of four to eight years.

Article 531

The authority or public official who, in a case involving an offence, orders, carries out or prolongs the incommunicado detention of a detainee, prisoner or convicted person, in violation of the time limits or other constitutional or legal guarantees, shall be punished with a penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of two to six years.

Article 532

If the acts described in the two preceding articles were committed through serious negligence, they shall be punished with a penalty of suspension from public employment or office for a period of six months to two years.

Article 533

The prison official or official of protection or correction centres for minors who imposes undue sanctions or deprivations on inmates or internees, or uses unnecessary rigour with them, shall be punished with a penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of two to six years.

Section 2

Offences committed by public officials against the inviolability of the home and other guarantees of privacy

Article 534

1. The authority or public official who, in a case involving an offence, and without respecting constitutional or legal guarantees, shall be punished with the penalties of a fine of six to twelve months and special disqualification from public employment or office for two to six years:
1. Enters a home without the consent of the occupant.
2. Searches the papers or documents of a person or the effects found in his home, unless the owner has freely given his consent.
If he does not return to the owner, immediately after the search, the papers, documents and effects searched, the penalties shall be those of special disqualification from public employment or office for six to twelve years and a fine of twelve to twenty-four months, without prejudice to the penalty that may correspond to him for the appropriation.
2. The authority or public official who, on the occasion of a lawful search of papers, documents or effects of a person, commits any unjust vexation or unnecessary damage to his property, shall be punished with the penalties provided for these acts, imposed in their upper half, and, in addition, with the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of two to six years.

Article 535

The authority or public official who, in a case involving an offence, intercepts any kind of private correspondence, postal or telegraphic, in violation of constitutional or legal guarantees, shall incur the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for two to six years.
If he discloses or reveals the information obtained, the penalty of special disqualification, in its upper half, and, in addition, that of a fine of six to eighteen months shall be imposed.

Article 536

The authority, public official or agent thereof who, in a case involving an offence, intercepts telecommunications or uses technical devices for listening, transmission,

recording or reproduction of sound, image or any other communication signal, in violation of constitutional or legal guarantees, shall incur the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for two to six years.
If he discloses or reveals the information obtained, the penalties of special disqualification, in its upper half and, in addition, that of a fine of six to eighteen months shall be imposed.

Section 3

Offences committed by public officials against other individual rights

Article 537

The authority or public official who prevents or obstructs the right to the assistance of a lawyer to the detainee or prisoner, procures or favours his waiver of such assistance or does not inform him immediately and in a manner comprehensible to him of his rights and the reasons for his detention, shall be punished with a penalty of a fine of four to ten months and special disqualification from public employment or office for two to four years.

Article 538

The authority or public official who establishes prior censorship or, outside the cases permitted by the Constitution and the Laws, collects editions of books or newspapers or suspends their publication or the broadcasting of any radio-television broadcast, shall incur the penalty of absolute disqualification for six to ten years.

Article 539

The authority or public official who dissolves or suspends in its activities a legally constituted association, without prior judicial decision, or without legitimate cause prevents it from holding its sessions, shall be punished with the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for eight to twelve years and a fine of six to twelve months.

Article 540

The authority or public official who prohibits a peaceful assembly or dissolves it outside the cases expressly permitted by the Laws, shall be punished with the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for four to eight years and a fine of six to nine months.

Article 541

The authority or public official who expropriates a person of their property outside the permitted cases and without complying with the legal requirements, shall incur the penalties of special disqualification from public employment or office for one to four years and a fine of six to twelve months.

Article 542

The authority or public official who, knowingly, prevents a person from exercising other civic rights recognised by the Constitution and the Laws shall incur the penalty of special disqualification from public employment or office for a period of one to four years.

CHAPTER VI

Outrages against Spain

Article 543

Offences or outrages by word, in writing or in deed against Spain, its Autonomous Communities or their symbols or emblems, carried out with publicity, shall be punished with a fine of seven to twelve months.

TITLE XXII

Offences against public order

CHAPTER I

Sedition

Article 544. (Repealed)

Article 545. (Repealed)

Article 546. (Repealed)

Article 547. (Repealed)

Article 548. (Repealed)

Article 549. (Repealed)

CHAPTER II

Attacks against authority, its agents and public officials, and resistance and disobedience

Article 550

1.	Those who assault or, with serious intimidation or violence, offer serious resistance to the authority, its agents or public officials, or attack them, when they are in the exercise of the functions of their offices or on the occasion thereof, are guilty of an attack.
In all cases, acts committed against teaching or health officials who are in the exercise of the functions proper to their office, or on the occasion thereof, shall be considered acts of attack.
2.	Attacks shall be punished with the penalties of imprisonment from one to four years and a fine of three to six months if the attack was against an authority and imprisonment from six months to three years in other cases.
3.	Notwithstanding the provisions of the preceding paragraph, if the authority against whom the attack was committed was a member of the Government, of the Governing Councils of the Autonomous Communities, of the Congress of Deputies, of the Senate or of the Legislative Assemblies of the Autonomous Communities, of local Corporations, of the General Council of the Judiciary, a Magistrate of the Constitutional Court, a judge, magistrate or member of the Public Prosecutor's Office, the penalty of imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of six to twelve months shall be imposed.

Article 551

The penalties higher in degree than those respectively provided for in the preceding article shall be imposed whenever the attack is committed:
1.	º By making use of weapons or other dangerous objects.
2.	º When the act of violence executed is potentially dangerous to the life of persons or may cause serious injuries. In particular, the cases of throwing blunt objects or flammable liquids, arson and the use of explosives are included.
3.	º By attacking the authority, its agent or the public official by making use of a motor vehicle.
4.	º When the acts are carried out on the occasion of a riot, mutiny or collective incident inside a prison establishment.

Article 552. (Repealed)

Article 553

Provocation, conspiracy and proposal for any of the offences provided for in the preceding articles shall be punished with the penalty lower by one or two degrees than that of the corresponding offence.

Article 554

1.	The acts described in Articles 550 and 551 shall also be punished with the penalties expressed therein when committed against a member of the Armed Forces who, wearing uniform, was providing a service that had been legally entrusted to him.
2.	The same penalties shall be imposed on those who attack, use violence or intimidate persons who come to the aid of the authority, its agents or officials.
3.	The penalties of Articles 550 and 551 shall also be imposed on those who attack, use violence or seriously intimidate:
a)	Firefighters or members of health personnel or rescue teams who were intervening on the occasion of a disaster, public calamity or emergency situation, with the purpose of preventing them from exercising their functions.
b)	Private security personnel, duly identified, who carry out private security activities in cooperation and under the command of the Security Forces and Corps.

Article 555. (Repealed)

Article 556

1.	Those who, without being included in Article 550, seriously resist or disobey the authority or its agents in the exercise of their functions, or private security personnel, duly identified, who carry out private security activities in cooperation and under the command of the Security Forces and Corps, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three months to one year or a fine of six to eighteen months.
2.	Those who fail to show the respect and consideration due to the authority, in the exercise of its functions, shall be punished with a fine of one to three months.

CHAPTER III

Public disorder

Article 557

1.	Those who, acting in a group and with the purpose of attacking public peace, execute acts of violence or intimidation shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from six months to three years:
a)	Against persons or property; or
b)	obstructing public thoroughfares causing a danger to the life or health of persons; or

c)	invading installations or buildings seriously altering the effective functioning of essential services in those places.
2.	The acts described in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from three to five years and special disqualification from public employment or office for the same period when committed by a multitude whose number, organisation and purpose are suitable to seriously affect public order. If the perpetrators are constituted as an authority, the penalty of disqualification shall be absolute for a period of six to eight years.
3.	The penalties of the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed in their upper half on participants who carried dangerous instruments or on those who carried out acts of pillage.
These penalties shall be applied one degree higher when firearms were carried.
4.	Provocation, conspiracy and proposal for the conduct provided for in paragraphs 2 and 3 of this article shall be punished with penalties lower by one or two degrees than those respectively provided for.
5.	Anyone who in a crowded place provokes an avalanche, stampede or other analogous reaction in the public that places the life or health of persons in a situation of danger shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from six months to two years.
6.	The penalties specified in this article shall be imposed without prejudice to those that may correspond to the specific acts of injury, threats, coercion or damage that have been carried out.

Article 557 bis

Those who, acting in a group, invade or occupy, against the will of its holder, the domicile of a public or private legal person, an office, establishment or premises, even if it is open to the public, and thereby cause a relevant disturbance of public peace and its normal activity, shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment from three to six months or a fine of six to twelve months, unless the acts were already punished with a more severe penalty in another provision of this Code.

Article 557 ter. (Repealed)

Article 558

Those who seriously disturb order in the hearing of a court or tribunal, in public acts of any authority or corporation, in an electoral college, public office or establishment, educational center or on the occasion of sporting or cultural events shall be punished with a prison sentence of three to six months or a fine of six to 12 months. In these cases, the penalty of deprivation of attendance at places, events or spectacles of the same nature for a period of up to three years longer than the prison sentence imposed may also be imposed.

Article 559. (Repealed)

Article 560

1.	Those who cause damage that interrupts, obstructs or destroys telecommunications lines or installations or postal correspondence shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years.
2.	The same penalty shall be incurred by those who cause damage to railway lines or cause serious harm to railway traffic in any of the ways provided for in Article 382.
3.	The same penalty shall be imposed on those who damage water, gas or electricity conduits or transmissions for populations, seriously interrupting or altering the supply or service.

Article 561

Whoever falsely asserts or simulates a situation of danger to the community or the occurrence of a disaster as a consequence of which it is necessary to provide assistance to another, and thereby causes the mobilization of police, assistance or rescue services, shall be punished with a prison sentence of three months and one day to one year or a fine of three to eighteen months.

CHAPTER IV

Common provision to the preceding chapters

Article 562

In the case that the person who commits any of the offenses set forth in the preceding chapters of this Title is constituted as an authority, the disqualification penalty that is provided for in each case shall be replaced by absolute disqualification for a period of ten to fifteen years, unless such circumstance is specifically contemplated in the criminal offense in question.

CHAPTER V

On the possession, trafficking and storage of weapons, ammunition or explosives

Article 563

The possession of prohibited weapons and those that are the result of substantial modification of the manufacturing characteristics of regulated weapons shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to three years.

Article 564

1.	The possession of regulated firearms, lacking the necessary licenses or permits, shall be punished:
1.	º With a prison sentence of one to two years, if they are short weapons.
2.	º With a prison sentence of six months to one year, if they are long weapons.
2. The offenses provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished, respectively, with prison sentences of two to three years and one to two years, when any of the following circumstances concur:
1.	ª That the weapons lack factory marks or numbers, or have them altered or erased.
2.	ª That they have been illegally introduced into Spanish territory.
3.	ª That they have been transformed, modifying their original characteristics.

Article 565

Judges or Courts may reduce by one degree the penalties indicated in the preceding articles, provided that the circumstances of the act and the offender demonstrate the lack of intention to use the weapons for illicit purposes.

Article 566

1.	Those who manufacture, market or establish deposits of weapons or ammunition not authorized by law or the competent authority shall be punished:
1.	º If they are weapons or ammunition of war or chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapons or anti-personnel mines or cluster munitions, with a prison sentence of five to ten years for promoters and organizers, and with a prison sentence of three to five years for those who have cooperated in their formation.

2.	º If they are regulated firearms or ammunition for them, with a prison sentence of two to four years for promoters and organizers, and with a prison sentence of six months to two years for those who have cooperated in their formation.
3.	º With the same penalties shall be punished, in their respective cases, the trafficking of weapons or ammunition of war or defense, or chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapons or anti-personnel mines or cluster munitions.
2.	The penalties contemplated in point 1.º of the preceding paragraph shall be imposed on those who develop or employ chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapons or anti-personnel mines or cluster munitions, or initiate military preparations for their use or do not destroy them in violation of international treaties or conventions to which Spain is a party.

Article 567

1.	The manufacture, marketing or possession of any of such weapons is considered a deposit of weapons of war, regardless of their model or class, even when they are in disassembled pieces. The manufacture, marketing or possession of the same is considered a deposit of chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapons or anti-personnel mines or cluster munitions.
The deposit of weapons, in its marketing aspect, includes both acquisition and disposal.
2.	Weapons of war are considered to be those determined as such in the provisions regulating national defense. Chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapons, anti-personnel mines or cluster munitions are considered to be those determined as such in international treaties or conventions to which Spain is a party.
Development of chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapons, anti-personnel mines or cluster munitions means any activity consisting of scientific or technical research or study aimed at creating a new chemical, biological, nuclear or radiological weapon, or anti-personnel mine or cluster munition or the modification of a pre-existing one.
3.	The manufacture, marketing or gathering of five or more of such weapons is considered a deposit of regulated firearms, even when they are in disassembled pieces.
4.	With respect to ammunition, Judges and Courts, taking into account the quantity and type thereof, shall declare whether they constitute a deposit for the purposes of this chapter.

Article 568

The possession or storage of explosive, flammable, incendiary or asphyxiating substances or devices, or their components, as well as their manufacture, trafficking or transport, or supply in any form, not authorized by law or the competent authority, shall be punished with a prison sentence of four to eight years, if they are its promoters and organizers, and with a prison sentence of three to five years for those who have cooperated in their formation.

Article 569

Deposits of weapons, ammunition or explosives established in the name or on behalf of an association with criminal purpose shall result in the judicial declaration of illegality and its consequent dissolution.

Article 570

1.	In the cases provided for in this chapter, the penalty of deprivation of the right to possession and carrying of weapons for a period three years longer than the prison sentence imposed may be imposed.
2.	Likewise, if the offender were authorized to manufacture or traffic in any of the substances, weapons and ammunition mentioned therein, he shall suffer, in addition to the penalties indicated, that of special disqualification for the exercise of his industry or trade for a period of 12 to 20 years.

CHAPTER VI

On criminal organizations and groups

Article 570 bis

1.	Those who promote, constitute, organize, coordinate or direct a criminal organization shall be punished with a prison sentence of four to eight years if it has as its purpose or object the commission of serious offences, and with a prison sentence of three to six years in other cases; and those who actively participate in the organization, form part of it or cooperate economically or in any other way with it shall be punished with prison sentences of two to five years if its aim is the commission of serious offences, and with a prison sentence of one to three years in other cases.
For the purposes of this Code, criminal organization means a grouping formed by more than two persons with a stable character or for an indefinite time, which in a concerted and coordinated manner distribute various tasks or functions with the aim of committing offences.
2.	The penalties provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be imposed in their upper half when the organization:
a)	is formed by a large number of persons.
b)	has weapons or dangerous instruments.
c)	has advanced technological means of communication or transport which by their characteristics are especially suitable for facilitating the execution of the offences or the impunity of the guilty parties.
If two or more of such circumstances concur, the penalties superior in degree shall be imposed.
3.	The penalties respectively provided for in this article shall be imposed in their upper half if the offences are against life or physical integrity of persons, liberty, sexual liberty and indemnity or trafficking in human beings.

Article 570 ter

1.	Those who constitute, finance or integrate a criminal group shall be punished:
a)	If the purpose of the group is to commit offences mentioned in paragraph 3 of the preceding article, with a sentence of two to four years' imprisonment if it concerns one or more serious offences and with a sentence of one to three years' imprisonment if it concerns less serious offences.
b)	With a sentence of six months to two years' imprisonment if the purpose of the group is to commit any other serious offence.
c)	With a sentence of three months to one year's imprisonment when it concerns committing one or more less serious offences not included in paragraph a) or the repeated perpetration of minor offences.
For the purposes of this Code, criminal group means the union of more than two persons which, without meeting one or some of the characteristics of the criminal organization defined in the preceding article, has as its purpose or object the concerted perpetration of offences.
2.	The penalties provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be imposed in their upper half when the group:
a)	is formed by a large number of persons.
b)	has weapons or dangerous instruments.
c)	has advanced technological means of communication or transport which by their characteristics are especially suitable for facilitating the execution of the offences or the impunity of the guilty parties.
If two or more of such circumstances concur, the penalties superior in degree shall be imposed.

Article 570 quater

1.	The judges or courts, in the cases provided for in this Chapter and the following one, shall order the dissolution of the organization or group and, where appropriate, any other of the consequences of articles 33.7 and 129 of this Code.
2.	Likewise, those responsible for the conduct described in the two preceding articles shall be imposed, in addition to the penalties provided for therein, special disqualification for all those economic activities or legal transactions related to the activity of the organization or criminal group or to their actions within them, for a time exceeding between six and twenty years that of the duration of the penalty of deprivation of liberty imposed where appropriate, attending proportionally to the gravity of the offence, the number of those committed and the circumstances concurring in the offender.
In any case, when the conduct provided for in said articles is covered by another provision of this Code, the provisions of rule
4.	ª of article 8 shall apply.
3.	The provisions of this Chapter shall be applicable to any criminal organization or group that carries out any criminally relevant act in Spain, even if they have been constituted, are based or develop their activity abroad.
4.	The judges or courts, giving reasons in the judgment, may impose on the person responsible for any of the offences provided for in this Chapter the penalty lower by one or two degrees, provided that the subject has voluntarily abandoned his criminal activities and has actively collaborated with the authorities or their agents, either to obtain decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other persons responsible or to prevent the action or development of the organizations or groups to which he has belonged, or to prevent the perpetration of an offence that was being attempted to be committed within or through said organizations or groups.

CHAPTER VII

On terrorist organizations and groups and offences of terrorism

Section 1.ª On terrorist organizations and groups

Article 571

For the purposes of this Code, terrorist organizations or groups shall be considered those groupings which, meeting the characteristics respectively established in the second paragraph of paragraph 1 of article 570 bis and in the second paragraph of paragraph 1 of article 570 ter, have as their purpose or object the commission of any of the offences classified in the following section.

Article 572

1.	Those who promote, constitute, organize or direct a terrorist organization or group shall be punished with prison sentences of eight to fifteen years and absolute disqualification during the time of the sentence.
2.	Those who actively participate in the organization or group, or form part of them, shall be punished with prison sentences of six to twelve years and absolute disqualification during the time of the sentence.

Section 2.ª On offences of terrorism

Article 573

1.	The commission of any serious offence against life or physical integrity, liberty, moral integrity, sexual liberty and indemnity, property, natural resources or the environment, public health, of catastrophic risk, fire, of documentary falsification, against the Crown, of attack and possession, trafficking and deposit of weapons, ammunition or explosives, provided for in the present Code, and the seizure of aircraft, ships or other means of collective transport or of goods, shall be considered an offence of terrorism when they are carried out with any of the following purposes:

1.	ª To subvert the constitutional order, or suppress or seriously destabilize the functioning of the political institutions or of the economic or social structures of the State, or compel the public authorities to perform an act or to refrain from doing so.
2.	ª To seriously alter public peace.
3.	ª To seriously destabilize the functioning of an international organization.
4.	ª To provoke a state of terror in the population or in a part of it.
2.	The computer offences classified in articles 197 bis and 197 ter and 264 to 264 quater shall likewise be considered offences of terrorism when the acts are committed with any of the purposes referred to in the preceding paragraph.
3.	Likewise, the rest of the offences classified in this Chapter shall be considered offences of terrorism.

Article 573 bis

1.	The terrorist offences referred to in paragraph 1 of the preceding article shall be punished with the following penalties:
1.	ª With imprisonment for the maximum time provided for in this Code if the death of a person is caused.
2.	ª With imprisonment of twenty to twenty-five years when, in cases of kidnapping or unlawful detention, no information is given as to the whereabouts of the person.
3.	ª With imprisonment of fifteen to twenty years if an abortion under article 144 is caused, injuries of those classified in articles 149, 150, 157 or 158 are produced, the kidnapping of a person, or devastation or fire of those provided for respectively in articles 346 and 351.
4.	ª With imprisonment of ten to fifteen years if any other injury is caused, or a person is unlawfully detained, threatened or coerced.
5.	ª And with the penalty provided for the offence committed in its upper half, it being possible to reach the higher degree, when it concerns any other of the offences referred to in paragraph 1 of the preceding article.
2.	The penalties shall be imposed in their upper half if the acts are committed against the persons mentioned in paragraph 3 of article 550 or against members of the Security Forces and Corps or of the Armed Forces or against public employees who provide service in penitentiary institutions.
3.	The terrorist offences referred to in paragraph 2 of the preceding article shall be punished with the penalty higher in degree than that respectively provided for in the corresponding articles.
4.	The offence of public disorder provided for in paragraphs 2 and 3 of article 557, as well as the offence of rebellion, when committed by a terrorist organisation or group or individually but under their protection, shall be punished with the penalty higher in degree than those provided for such offences.

Article 574

1.	The deposit of weapons or ammunition, the possession or deposit of explosive, flammable, incendiary or asphyxiating substances or devices, or of their components, as well as their manufacture, trafficking, transport or supply in any form, and the mere placement or use of such substances or of the appropriate means or devices, shall be punished with imprisonment of eight to fifteen years when the acts are committed with any of the purposes expressed in paragraph 1 of article 573.
2.	The penalty of ten to twenty years' imprisonment shall be imposed when it concerns nuclear, radiological, chemical or biological weapons, substances or devices, or any others of similar destructive power.
3.	Those who, with the same purposes indicated in paragraph 1, develop chemical or biological weapons, or seize, possess, transport, provide to others or manipulate nuclear materials, radioactive elements or materials or equipment producing ionising radiation shall also be punished with imprisonment of ten to twenty years.

Article 575

1.	Anyone who, for the purpose of training themselves to carry out any of the offences classified in this Chapter, receives indoctrination or military or combat training, or in techniques for developing chemical or biological weapons, for elaborating or preparing explosive, flammable, incendiary or asphyxiating substances or devices, or specifically intended to facilitate the commission of any such infractions, shall be punished with imprisonment of two to five years.
2.	Anyone who, with the same purpose of training themselves to commit any of the offences classified in this Chapter, carries out by themselves any of the activities provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be punished with the same penalty.
It shall be understood that anyone who, with such purpose, habitually accesses one or more communication services accessible to the public online or content accessible through the internet or an electronic communications service whose contents are directed at or are suitable for inciting incorporation into a terrorist organisation or group, or to collaborate with any of them or in their aims, commits this offence. The acts shall be understood as committed in Spain when the contents are accessed from Spanish territory.
It shall likewise be understood that anyone who, with the same purpose, acquires or has in their possession documents that are directed at or, by their content, are suitable for inciting incorporation into a terrorist organisation or group or to collaborate with any of them or in their aims, commits this offence.
3.	The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who, for that same purpose, or to collaborate with a terrorist organisation or group, or to commit any of the offences included in this Chapter, travels to or establishes themselves in a foreign territory.

Article 576

1.	Anyone who, by any means, directly or indirectly, solicits, acquires, possesses, uses, converts, transmits or carries out any other activity with goods or assets of any kind with the intention that they be used, or knowing that they will be used, in whole or in part, to commit any of the offences included in this Chapter, shall be punished with imprisonment of five to ten years and a fine of three to five times their value.
2.	If the goods or assets are effectively placed at the disposal of the person responsible for the terrorist offence, the penalty higher in degree may be imposed. If they come to be used for the execution of specific terrorist acts, the act shall be punished as co-authorship or complicity, as the case may be.
3.	In the event that the conduct referred to in paragraph 1 has been carried out by acting against property, committing extortion, documentary forgery or through the commission of any other offence, these shall be punished with the penalty higher in degree than that which corresponds to them, without prejudice to also imposing that which is appropriate pursuant to the preceding paragraphs.
4.	Anyone who, being specifically subject by law to collaborate with the authority in the prevention of terrorist financing activities, gives rise, through serious negligence in the fulfilment of such obligations, to any of the conducts described in paragraph 1 not being detected or prevented, shall be punished with the penalty one or two degrees lower than that provided for therein.

Article 577

1.	Anyone who carries out, seeks or facilitates any act of collaboration with the activities or purposes of a terrorist organisation, group or element, or to commit any of the offences included in this Chapter, shall be punished with imprisonment from five to ten years and a fine of eighteen to twenty-four months.
In particular, acts of collaboration include information or surveillance of persons, property or installations, the construction, conditioning, transfer or use of accommodation or storage facilities, the concealment, harbouring or transportation of persons, the organisation of training practices or attendance at them, the provision of technological services, and any other equivalent form of cooperation or assistance to the activities of the terrorist organisations or groups, groups or persons referred to in the preceding paragraph.

When the information or surveillance of persons mentioned in the preceding paragraph endangers their life, physical integrity, freedom or property, the penalty provided for in this section shall be imposed in its upper half. If injury to any of these legal interests occurs, the act shall be punished as co-authorship or complicity, as the case may be.
2.	The penalties provided for in the preceding section shall be imposed on those who carry out any activity of recruitment, indoctrination or training, which is directed or which, by its content, is suitable to incite incorporation into a terrorist organisation or group, or to commit any of the offences included in this Chapter.
These penalties shall also be imposed on those who provide training or instruction on the manufacture or use of explosives, firearms or other weapons or harmful or dangerous substances, or on methods or techniques especially suitable for the commission of any of the offences in Article 573, with the intention or knowledge that they will be used for that purpose.
The penalties shall be imposed in their upper half, and may reach the higher degree, when the acts provided for in this section have been directed at minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection or at women victims of trafficking for the purpose of converting them into spouses, partners or sexual slaves of the perpetrators of the offence, without prejudice to imposing those that are also appropriate for offences against sexual freedom committed.
3.	If collaboration with the activities or purposes of a terrorist organisation or group, or in the commission of any of the offences included in this Chapter, has occurred through gross negligence, the penalty of imprisonment from six to eighteen months and a fine of six to twelve months shall be imposed.

Article 578

1.	The public glorification or justification of the offences included in Articles 572 to 577 or of those who have participated in their execution, or the performance of acts that entail discredit, contempt or humiliation of the victims of terrorist offences or their relatives, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years and a fine of twelve to eighteen months. The judge may also order in the sentence, for the period of time that he himself determines, any one or more of the prohibitions provided for in Article 57.
2.	The penalties provided for in the preceding section shall be imposed in their upper half when the acts have been carried out through the dissemination of services or content accessible to the public through the media, the internet, or by means of electronic communications services or through the use of information technologies.
3.	When the acts, in view of their circumstances, are suitable to seriously disturb public peace or create a serious feeling of insecurity or fear in society or part of it, the penalty shall be imposed in its upper half, which may be raised to the higher degree.
4.	The judge or court shall order the destruction, erasure or rendering unusable of books, files, documents, articles or any other medium by which the offence has been committed. When the offence has been committed through information and communication technologies, the removal of the content shall be ordered.
If the acts have been committed through services or content accessible through the internet or electronic communications services, the judge or court may order the removal of the unlawful content or services. Subsidiarily, it may order hosting service providers to remove the unlawful content, search engines to delete links pointing to it and electronic communications service providers to block access to the unlawful content or services whenever any of the following circumstances exists:
a)	When the measure is proportionate to the seriousness of the acts and the relevance of the information and necessary to prevent its dissemination.
b)	When the content referred to in the preceding sections is disseminated exclusively or predominantly.

5.	The measures provided for in the preceding section may also be ordered by the investigating judge as a precautionary measure during the investigation of the case.

Article 579

1.	Anyone who, by any means, publicly disseminates messages or slogans that have the purpose or which, by their content, are suitable to incite others to commit any of the offences in this Chapter shall be punished with a penalty one or two degrees lower than that provided for the offence in question.
2.	The same penalty shall be imposed on anyone who, publicly or before a gathering of persons, incites others to commit any of the offences in this Chapter, as well as anyone who solicits another person to commit them.
3.	Other acts of provocation, conspiracy and proposal to commit any of the offences regulated in this Chapter shall also be punished with a penalty one or two degrees lower than that corresponding respectively to the acts provided for in this Chapter.
4.	In the cases provided for in this provision, judges or courts may adopt the measures established in sections 4 and 5 of the preceding article.

Article 579 bis

1. The person responsible for the offences provided for in this Chapter, without prejudice to the penalties that correspond in accordance with the preceding articles, shall also be punished, taking into account proportionally the gravity of the offence, the number of those committed and the circumstances present in the offender, with the penalties of absolute disqualification, special disqualification from educational profession or occupation, in the teaching, sports and leisure spheres, for a period exceeding by between six and twenty years the duration of the custodial sentence imposed, where applicable, in the judgment.
2. A person convicted of a serious custodial sentence for one or more offences included in this Chapter shall also be subject to the measure of supervised release from five to ten years, and from one to five years if the custodial sentence were less serious. Notwithstanding the foregoing, when it concerns a single offence that is not serious, and its perpetrator had offended for the first time, the court may or may not impose the measure of supervised release, having regard to his lesser dangerousness.
3. In the offences provided for in this Chapter, the judges and courts, giving reasons in the judgment, may impose the penalty one or two degrees lower than that prescribed for the offence in question, when the subject has voluntarily abandoned his criminal activities, presents himself to the authorities confessing the facts in which he has participated and actively collaborates with them to prevent the commission of the offence, or effectively contributes to obtaining decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other persons responsible or to prevent the action or development of organisations, groups or other terrorist elements to which he has belonged or with which he has collaborated.
4. The judges and courts, with reasons given, having regard to the specific circumstances, may also impose the penalty one or two degrees lower than that prescribed in this Chapter for the offence in question, when the act is objectively of lesser gravity, having regard to the means employed or the result produced.

Article 580

In all terrorism offences, the conviction of a foreign judge or court shall be treated as equivalent to the judgments of Spanish judges or courts for the purposes of applying the aggravating circumstance of recidivism.

Article 580 bis

When in accordance with the provisions of Article 31 bis a legal person is responsible for the offences contained in this Chapter, the following penalties shall be imposed:
a) Fine of two to five years, or double to quadruple the damage caused when the resulting amount is higher, if the offence committed by the natural person carries a penalty of more than two years' deprivation of liberty.

b) Fine of six months to two years, or double to triple the damage caused if the resulting amount is higher, in other cases.
Having regard to the rules established in Article 66 bis, the judges and courts may also impose the penalties contained in letters b) to g) of paragraph 7 of Article 33.

TITLE XXIII

Offences of treason and against the peace or independence of the State and relating to National Defence

CHAPTER I

Offences of treason

Article 581

A Spaniard who induces a foreign power to declare war on Spain or conspires with it for the same purpose, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years.

Article 582

The following shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from twelve to twenty years:
1. A Spaniard who facilitates the enemy's entry into Spain, the taking of a square, military post, vessel or aircraft of the State or stores of supplies or armament.
2. A Spaniard who seduces or recruits Spanish troops or troops in the service of Spain, so that they go over to the enemy ranks or desert their flags while on campaign.
3. A Spaniard who recruits people or supplies arms or other effective means to wage war against Spain, under enemy flags.

Article 583

The following shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from twelve to twenty years:
1. A Spaniard who takes up arms against the Homeland under enemy flags.
The penalty one degree higher shall be imposed on one who acts as leader or promoter, or holds any command, or is constituted in authority.
2. A Spaniard who supplies enemy troops with funds, arms, vessels, aircraft, effects or munitions of supplies or armament or other direct and effective means to attack Spain, or favours the progress of enemy arms in a manner not included in the preceding article.
3. A Spaniard who supplies the enemy with plans of fortresses, buildings or terrain, documents or information that directly lead to the same purpose of attacking Spain or favouring the progress of enemy arms.
4. A Spaniard who, in time of war, prevents national troops from receiving the aid referred to in number 2 or the data and information indicated in number 3 of this article.

Article 584

A Spaniard who, with the purpose of favouring a foreign power, association or international organisation, procures, falsifies, renders useless or discloses information classified as reserved or secret, capable of prejudicing national security or national defence, shall be punished, as a traitor, with the penalty of imprisonment from six to twelve years.

Article 585

Incitement, conspiracy and proposal for any of the offences provided for in the preceding articles of this chapter, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment one or two degrees lower than that of the corresponding offence.

Article 586

A foreigner resident in Spain who commits any of the offences included in this chapter shall be punished with the penalty one degree lower than that prescribed for them, except as established by Treaties or by the Law of Nations concerning diplomatic, consular and international organisation officials.

Article 587

The penalties prescribed in the preceding articles of this chapter are applicable to those who commit the offences included therein against a power allied with Spain, in the event of being on campaign against the common enemy.

Article 588

Members of the Government who, without complying with the provisions of the Constitution, declare war or sign peace shall incur the penalty of imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years.

CHAPTER II

Offences that compromise the peace or independence of the State

Article 589

Whoever publishes or executes in Spain any order, provision or document of a foreign Government that attacks the independence or security of the State, opposes the observance of its Laws or provokes their breach, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from one to three years.

Article 590

1. Whoever, by illegal acts or acts that are not duly authorised, provokes or gives cause for a declaration of war against Spain by another power, or exposes Spaniards to suffering vexations or reprisals in their persons or property, shall be punished with the penalty of imprisonment from eight to fifteen years if an authority or official, and from four to eight if not.
2. If the war does not come to be declared nor the vexations or reprisals take effect, the immediately lower penalty shall be imposed, respectively.

Article 591

With the same penalties prescribed in the preceding article shall be punished, in their respective cases, whoever, during a war in which Spain does not intervene, executes any act that compromises the neutrality of the State or infringes the provisions published by the Government to maintain it.

Article 592

1. Those who, with the purpose of harming the authority of the State or compromising the dignity or vital interests of Spain, maintain intelligence or relations of any kind with foreign Governments, with their agents or with international or foreign groups, Organizations or Associations, shall be punished with a prison sentence of four to eight years.
2. Whoever carries out the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph with the intention of provoking a war or rebellion shall be punished in accordance with articles 581, 473 or 475 of this Code as applicable.

Article 593

A prison sentence of eight to fifteen years shall be imposed on whoever violates a truce or armistice agreed between the Spanish Nation and another enemy nation, or between their belligerent forces.

Article 594

1. A Spaniard who, in time of war, communicates or circulates false news or rumours aimed at harming the credit of the State or the interests of the Nation, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to two years.
2. A foreigner who carries out any of the acts included in the preceding paragraph in Spanish territory shall incur the same penalties.

Article 595

Whoever, without legally granted authorization, raises troops in Spain for the service of a foreign power, whatever the objective pursued or the Nation intended to be attacked, shall be punished with a prison sentence of four to eight years.

Article 596

1. Whoever, in time of war and with the purpose of compromising the peace, security or independence of the State, has correspondence with an enemy country or one occupied by its troops when the Government has prohibited it, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to five years. If in the correspondence warnings or news are given from which the enemy could benefit, a prison sentence of eight to fifteen years shall be imposed.
2. Whoever commits the offences included in this article shall incur the same penalties, even if the correspondence is directed through a friendly or neutral country to evade the Law.
3. If the offender intended to serve the enemy with his warnings or news, he shall be deemed to be included in number 3 or number 4 of article 583.

Article 597

A Spaniard or foreigner who, being in national territory, passes or attempts to pass to an enemy country when the Government has prohibited it, shall be punished with a fine of six to twelve months.

CHAPTER III

Discovery and disclosure of secrets and information relating to National Defence

Article 598

Whoever, without the purpose of favouring a foreign power, obtains, discloses, falsifies or renders useless information legally classified as reserved or secret, related to national security or national defence or relating to the technical means or systems employed by the Armed Forces or industries of military interest, shall be punished with a prison sentence of one to four years.

Article 599

The penalty established in the preceding article shall be applied in its upper half when any of the following circumstances concur:
1. º That the active subject is a custodian or has knowledge of the secret or information by reason of his office or assignment.
2. º That the disclosure consists of publicising the secret or information in any media outlet or in a manner that ensures its dissemination.

Article 600

1. Whoever without express authorization reproduces plans or documentation referring to military zones, installations or materials that are of restricted access and whose knowledge is protected and reserved by information legally classified as reserved or secret, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years.
2. Whoever has in his possession objects or information legally classified as reserved or secret, relating to national security or national defence, without complying with the provisions established in current legislation, shall be punished with the same penalty.

Article 601

Whoever, by reason of his office, commission or service, has in his possession or officially knows of objects or information legally classified as reserved or secret or of military interest, relating to national security or national defence, and through serious negligence causes them to be known by an unauthorized person or disclosed, published or rendered useless, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to one year.

Article 602

Whoever discovers, violates, discloses, removes or uses information legally classified as reserved or secret related to nuclear energy, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years, unless the act has a more severe penalty assigned in another Law.

Article 603

Whoever destroys, renders useless, falsifies or opens without authorization correspondence or documentation legally classified as reserved or secret, related to national defence and which he has in his possession by reason of his office or assignment, shall be punished with a prison sentence of two to five years and special disqualification from employment or public office for a period of three to six years.

Article 604

(Without content)

TITLE XXIV

Offences against the International Community

CHAPTER I

Offences against the Law of nations

Article 605

1. Whoever kills the Head of a foreign State, or another person internationally protected by a Treaty, who is in Spain, shall be punished with a reviewable permanent prison sentence.
2. Whoever causes injuries of those provided for in article 149 to the persons mentioned in the preceding paragraph, shall be punished with a prison sentence of fifteen to twenty years.
If it were any of the injuries provided for in article 150, it shall be punished with a prison sentence of eight to fifteen years, and four to eight years if it were any other injury.
3. Any other offence committed against the persons mentioned in the preceding numbers, or against the official premises, private residence or means of transport of such persons, shall be punished with the penalties established in this Code for the respective offences, in their upper half.

Article 606

1. Whoever violates the personal immunity of the Head of another State or of another person internationally protected by a Treaty, shall be punished with a prison sentence of six months to three years.
2. When the offences included in this article and in the preceding one do not have a reciprocal penalty assigned in the laws of the country to which the offended persons belong, the offender shall be imposed the penalty that would be appropriate for the offence, in accordance with the provisions of this Code, if the offended person did not have the official character mentioned in the preceding paragraph.

CHAPTER II

Offences of genocide

Article 607

1. Those who, with the purpose of destroying totally or partially a national, ethnic, racial, religious group or one determined by the disability of its members, perpetrate any of the following acts, shall be punished:
1. º With the penalty of reviewable permanent imprisonment, if they kill any of its members.
2. º With the penalty of reviewable permanent imprisonment, if they sexually assault any of its members or cause any of the injuries provided for in article 149.
3. º With the penalty of imprisonment from eight to fifteen years, if they subject the group or any of its individuals to conditions of existence that endanger their life or seriously disturb their health, or when they cause any of the injuries provided for in article 150.
4. º With the same penalty, if they carry out forced displacements of the group or its members, adopt any measure that tends to prevent their way of life or reproduction, or forcibly transfer individuals from one group to another.
5. º With imprisonment from four to eight years, if they cause any other injury different from those indicated in numerals 2.º and 3.º of this section.
2. In all cases, the penalty of special disqualification for educational profession or occupation, in the teaching, sports and leisure fields, shall also be imposed for a period between three and five years longer than the duration of the penalty of deprivation of liberty imposed where applicable in the sentence, taking proportionally into account the seriousness of the crime and the circumstances present in the offender.

CHAPTER II BIS

Crimes against humanity

Article 607 bis

1. Those who commit the acts provided for in the following section as part of a widespread or systematic attack against the civilian population or against a part of it are guilty of crimes against humanity.
In any case, the commission of such acts shall be considered a crime against humanity:
1. º By reason of the victim's belonging to a group or collective persecuted for political, racial, national, ethnic, cultural, religious, gender, disability or other reasons universally recognized as unacceptable under international law.
2. º In the context of an institutionalized regime of systematic oppression and domination of one racial group over one or more racial groups and with the intention of maintaining that regime.
2. Those guilty of crimes against humanity shall be punished:
1. º With the penalty of reviewable permanent imprisonment if they cause the death of any person.

2. º With the penalty of imprisonment from 12 to 15 years if they commit rape, and from four to six years of imprisonment if the act consists of any other sexual assault.
3. º With the penalty of imprisonment from 12 to 15 years if they cause any of the injuries of article 149, and with imprisonment from eight to 12 years if they subject persons to conditions of existence that endanger their life or seriously disturb their health or when they cause any of the injuries provided for in article 150. The penalty of imprisonment from four to eight years shall be applied if they commit any of the injuries of article 147.
4. º With the penalty of imprisonment from eight to 12 years if they deport or forcibly transfer, without reasons authorized by international law, one or more persons to another State or place, by means of expulsion or other coercive acts.
5. º With the penalty of imprisonment from six to eight years if they force the pregnancy of any woman with the intention of modifying the ethnic composition of the population, without prejudice to the penalty that may correspond, where applicable, for other crimes.
6. º With the penalty of imprisonment from twelve to fifteen years for forced disappearance of persons. Forced disappearance shall be understood as the apprehension, detention or abduction or any other form of deprivation of liberty carried out by agents of the State or by persons or groups of persons acting with the authorization, support or acquiescence of the State, followed by a refusal to acknowledge said deprivation of liberty or the concealment of the fate or whereabouts of the disappeared person, removing them from the protection of the law.
7. º With the penalty of imprisonment from eight to 12 years if they detain another, depriving them of their liberty, in violation of international norms on detention.
The penalty one degree lower shall be imposed when the detention lasts less than fifteen days.
8. º With the penalty of four to eight years of imprisonment if they commit serious torture on persons under their custody or control, and with imprisonment from two to six years if it is less serious.
For the purposes of this article, torture is understood as subjecting the person to physical or psychological suffering.
The penalty provided for in this number shall be imposed without prejudice to the penalties that may correspond, where applicable, for attacks against other rights of the victim.
9. º With the penalty of imprisonment from four to eight years if they commit any of the conducts relating to prostitution contained in article 187.1, and with imprisonment from six to eight years in the cases provided for in article 188.1.
The penalty of imprisonment from six to eight years shall be imposed on those who transfer persons from one place to another, for the purpose of their sexual exploitation, using violence, intimidation or deception, or abusing a situation of superiority or need or vulnerability of the victim.
When the conducts provided for in the preceding paragraph and in article 188.1 are committed against minors or persons with disabilities in need of special protection, the penalties one degree higher shall be imposed.
10. º With the penalty of imprisonment from four to eight years if they subject any person to slavery or maintain them in it. This penalty shall be applied without prejudice to those that, where applicable, may correspond for the specific attacks committed against the rights of persons.
Slavery shall be understood as the situation of the person over whom another exercises, even in fact, all or some of the attributes of the right of ownership, such as buying, selling, lending or bartering them.
3.
